deterministic sort tie-break; `point(at:)` periodic; exact at full terms (`EpicyclesTests`). **Classic curves** — `phyllotaxis` / `lissajous` / `rose` (parity-dependent closure span, no retraced doubling) / `hypotrochoid`/`epitrochoid` (integer gears, exactly `wheel/gcd` laps) / `superellipse` (Lamé identity pinned per point; uniform-angle sampling, `resampled(spacing:)` for plotter-even outlines at extreme `n`) / `supershape` (2D Gielis mirroring `Mesh.supershape`'s defaults + its clamp-to-4 radius guard; **integer `m` or the curve doesn't close in one turn** — a fractional `m` leaves a seam, caught in the example) / `guilloche` + `Rosette` (rose-engine rings: stacked sinusoid cams over evenly spaced bases, ring k rotated by k·twist — the twist is a *pure rotation* of the wave and stacked rosettes *superpose*, both pinned exactly in `GuillocheTests`; snapshot `guilloche`, example `Patterns/Guilloche`) / the damped-pendulum `Harmonograph` / Chaikin `smoothed(iterations:)` (open contours keep exact endpoints); all rng-free; examples `Shapes/Superellipse` + `Shapes/Supershape`. `Docs/Drawing/Curves.md`. **Shape morphing** — `ShapeMorph` (build once, read `shape(at:)`) + `Shape`/`Contour: Tweenable` + `morphed(toward:_:spacing:)`; orientation mismatch reverses **all** target contours (never per-pair); spacing derives per outline, **not** per pair; unmatched contours lerp to their own centroid; originals verbatim at 0/1 (`ShapeMorphTests`). `Docs/Drawing/Morphing.md`. **Voronoi & Delaunay** (Bowyer–Watson + clip to `bounds`, `relaxed()` Lloyd) + **blue noise** (Bridson `poissonDisk`, a public generic over any `RandomNumberGenerator`). **Low-discrepancy sampling** — `halton` / `haltonPoints` / `sobolPoints`: pure index functions (no rng, never touch the sketch's `rng`); `startIndex` defaults **1**; Halton bases stay **coprime** (`SamplingTests`). **Stippling** — Secord weighted-Voronoi `stipple`; the ring search may stop before ring r only once the best find is ≤ **(r−1)·bucketSide** (the r·bucketSide bound was a real lattice-locking bug); zero ink → **empty array**; density = (1 − linear `luminance`) · alpha (`StippleTests`). **Random walks** — `randomWalk` / `levyFlight` (`minStep` > 0 — the power law diverges at zero) / `selfAvoidingWalk` (visited-forever marks + parent-link reconstruction); all seeded (`WalkTests`). **Truchet** — arcs centered on cell *corners*, so abutting cells join under any flip. **Hitomezashi** — one phase bit per grid line (shorter bit arrays *cycle*; seeded form draws bits at `probability`); two faces of one design: `stitches` (open 2-point `Contour`s, same-line dashes never touch) + `parities` (the two-coloring, row-major zip with `grid.cells` — tone boundaries land exactly on stitches, `HitomezashiTests` pins the theorem geometrically, sabotage-verified); `Docs/Drawing/Hitomezashi.md`, example `Patterns/Hitomezashi`, snapshot `hitomezashi`. **Tiling & layout** — `HexGrid`/`TriangleGrid` (true-aspect blocks, never stretch; axial math; `cell(at:)` via **cube rounding**), `Subdivision` (the root always splits; `chance` from depth 1), `Maze` (three carvers, all perfect; collinear walls merged; double-BFS `longestPath()`), `apollonianGasket` (Descartes/Vieta other-root — linear recursion); `TilingTests`. `Docs/Drawing/Tiling.md`. **Aperiodic tilings** (`Geometry/Penrose.swift` / `WangTiles.swift` / `Girih.swift` / `Spectre.swift`) — `penroseTiling` (P2/P3 by Robinson half-tile deflation from a ten-half wheel, merged into whole tiles; **the P2 child orderings encode each half's axis leg** — wrong assignments T-junction at depth 2, crack-free-pinned; **rhomb outlines lead with the positively-wound half's apex** — the intrinsic orientation the arc decoration needs, since C2-symmetric arc placement collapses to midpoint crossings (a solved dead end, not a tuning knob); arc fractions are solved continuity constants; rng-free); `wangTiling` (scanline fill honoring west/north edges; `WangTiling.completeSet` never dead-ends, arbitrary sets retry then return nil; weighted picks off the sketch rng); `girihPattern` (Hankin/Kaplan inference: two rays per edge midpoint at the contact angle, paired greedily by summed length with `(cost, i, j)` tie-breaks; unmatched rays drop; `Girih.Tile.points(onEdge:_:)` is the exact patch-composition primitive); `spectreTiling` (the chiral-monotile paper's 9-metatile / 8-slot substitution; **every generation mirrors whole**, so leaves at any depth share one handedness — pinned by the cyclic turn-sequence test; gamma carries the 30°-rotated `isOdd` mystic; `curve:` bends edges into the strictly chiral spectre, alternation cyclically consistent because 14 is even; rng-free). All four crack-free-tested (`AperiodicTilingTests`); examples `Patterns/Penrose` / `WangTiles` / `Girih` / `Spectre`; snapshots `penrose` / `wang-tiles` / `girih` / `spectre`. `Docs/Drawing/AperiodicTilings.md`. **Hyperbolic tiling** — `hyperbolicTiling(sides:meeting:)` / `HyperbolicTiling.tiles`, any {p,q} with (p−2)(q−2) > 4 in the Poincaré disk: the central polygon placed at the exact derived vertex radius (cosh = cot(π/p)·cot(π/q)), grown breadth-first by geodesic-circle inversion with center-dedup, pruned by drawn `minEdge`; tiles carry `depth` and a `parity` two-coloring (checkerboards iff `meeting` is even), and `viewpoint` re-seats the tiling *before* pruning so a pan is a rigid motion (tile count stays ~constant — the test pins panned tiles as exact images of centered ones, the pin that caught a conjugate-sign bug the horizon checks missed). Deterministic, rng-free. Example `Patterns/HyperbolicTiling`; snapshot `hyperbolic-tiling`; `HyperbolicTilingTests` (7, the pan pin sabotage-verified red). `Docs/Drawing/HyperbolicTiling.md`. **Circle & shape packing** — grow-to-touch `packCircles` + closed-form `packCircles(around:)` + `relaxCircles` (fixed-axis parting); geometry-aware `packShapes` over the stateful `ContinuousPacking` (fits actual outlines); an unbounded `maxRadius` clamps the grid cell **and** reach to the region size (`PackingTests`). **L-systems** — 13 verified presets; 2M-symbol expansion cap; one `Contour` per unbroken turtle run (a branch shares its branch point with the trunk). **Parametric L-systems** (`ParametricLSystem` + `LSystemExpression`/`*Parsing`/`*Turtle`/`*Presets`, ABOP §1.10 + Hanan 1992 ch. 4) — modules carrying reals, rules doing arithmetic; rules are **text in the literature's notation** (`A(s) : s > 0.02 -> F(s)[+A(s/2)] : weight`). **Load-bearing:** the match key is **(letter, arity)** and an unmatched module **stands for itself** (which is both why turtle symbols need no rules and how a guarded form *settles*); productions are a **source-ordered array** and **first-match-wins**, but **first-match and weighted normalisation are mutually exclusive policies**, so the engine gates on whether *any* rule declares a weight (weights are shared over the rules that matched, so disjoint guards behave identically either way); Hanan's precedence table is **not C's** — `^` binds **above** `*` and groups **right**, unary binds above `^` (`-2^2 == 4`), and getting it wrong is silently wrong rather than a parse error; names resolve to **positions at parse time** (evaluation touches no dictionary); the word is **flat SoA** (`letters`/`bounds`/`values`, ASCII `UInt8`) since a published derivation runs to millions of modules and a per-module `[Double]` would spend the expansion in the allocator; `==` stays **exact** (the sanctioned idiom is an integer counter guarded `d > 0` / `d == 0`) and a **NaN condition reads false** (`NaN != 0` is true in IEEE, so a plain zero-test would fire a rule on a division that went wrong); a rule that will not parse is **recorded in `errors` + noted once and left out**, never thrown or trapped. **The turtle is the plain one plus three things:** per-symbol parameters (**first parameter steers, extras ignored** — what lets `F(x,t)` draw `x` while `t` counts down), a **width** `!(w)` that `[`/`]` save and restore (claimed onto a branch's opening point, so `!(w)F(l)` is uniform), and `%` cut. **A roll or pitch of a whole number of half-turns is planar** — it is a **handedness flip** (`+`/`-` reverse), which is what makes 6 of the published 9-tree rows exactly faithful rather than 2; every other roll/pitch and the polygon/colour/surface symbols note once and draw nothing. `+` follows the **shipped 2D turtle's** sense (mirrors the book's figures; consistency with `LSystem` beats matching the page). Widths reach the canvas as **`StrokeMark`s normalised so the widest is 1** (so `strokeWeight` = the trunk), via `lSystemMarks` / `drawLSystem(…, tapered:)`; `contours` and `marks` come off **one walk** so they cannot drift. `LSystem.fitTransform` is shared by both kinds (extracted with identical arithmetic — 185/185 snapshots unmoved). **Two turn angles are Ollin's, not the sources'** (compound leaves 45°, mesotonic) and say so in the preset docs + `ATTRIBUTION.md`. Deliberate cut: **context-sensitive** productions (`A < B > C`), a clean published boundary — the plain `LSystem` has no context either. Example `Patterns/ParametricLSystem`; snapshot `parametric-l-system`; `ParametricLSystemTests` (49, eight mechanisms each verified red by sabotage). **Differential growth** — stateful class, order-stable → a fixed-`frame` snapshot reproduces. **WFC** — simple-tiled (Gumin); sorted candidates + fixed cell order or the seeded solve flakes. **Overlapping WFC** (`OverlappingWFC` + `wfc(from:)`/`wfc(_ model:)`) learns its constraints from an example `Image` instead of declared tiles (`patternSize` / `WFCSymmetry` / `wrapsSample` / `tileable`), synthesizing a larger texture whose every NxN square is one the sample held; six invariants are load-bearing, each a way it goes *wrong* rather than slow: propagation runs the **four unit offsets only** (sound by transitivity through the intervening cell); the **support counter `compatible[cell][p][d]` counts the neighbor OPPOSITE `d`** (so it inits to `propagatorCount[opposite[d]][p]` — using `d` compiles and is quietly wrong) and `ban` zeroes all four so nothing bans twice; **entropy is Shannon over the weights** (a plain survivor count can't tell a 1000:1 cell from a 50:50 one) with an `.infinity` sentinel; a bounded output's last **N-1 columns/rows are outside the solve entirely** (never observed, never propagated into — a ban spreading there reads as a false contradiction) and the read-out recovers those pixels from the last covering cell, so an output needs **2N-2** a side; **patterns with no legal neighbor in a direction that exists are pre-banned** (propagation can never eliminate one, so it survives to be chosen and the output goes quietly *illegal*, the bounded-sample case); determinism per the catalog rule. Deliberate cuts: no seeding constraints (upstream's `ground` names a pattern by *index*, an artifact of extraction order) and no backtracking (restart, `attempts` times). Envelope stated not faked: pixel-art samples only (exact color equality; caps at 256 colors / 1024 patterns), solves fail above ~50px a side (NP-hard), and `tileable` is *unsatisfiable* when the sample's period doesn't divide the output size. Example `Patterns/TextureSynthesis`; snapshot `texture-synthesis`; `OverlappingWFCTests`. **1D CA & turmites** — `elementaryCA` / `totalisticCA` (pure functions; the Sketch facade mirrors the full free-function signatures — the shadowing rule) + `Turmite` (published turn codes, no rng, `Preset` catalog; `CellularAutomataTests`). `Docs/Generators/CellularAutomata.md`. **Flow fields** — `FlowField` + Jobard-Lefer `streamlines` (a batch commits to the occupancy grid only *after* tracing, so a line never blocks itself); the seeded sugar captures the sketch's noise → transient, don't store the field (it retains `self`). **Boids & steering** — `Boids` (order-stable force sums → seeded flocks reproduce) + `Vehicle` (behaviors *return* forces; only `wander` rolls — a unit roll scaled by jitter; arc-length path follow, `closed:` wraps; `SteeringTests`). **Force-directed graph layout** (`Geometry/ForceLayout.swift`) — `ForceLayout` + `drawGraph`, the FR spring embedder (repel `k²/d` all pairs, pull `d²/k` on edges, temperature-capped moves clamped to bounds, linear cooling); **`idealDistance` is a scale dial, not a promised edge length** (a ring's equilibrium spacing sits well under k — measured on-equilibrium, so raise k to open the web, never iterate longer) and **a settled layout is frozen** (`step()` early-returns; `reheat(_:)` is the one path back into motion — the growth/drag idiom); pinned nodes hold, `gravity` (default 0) centers disconnected pieces, coincident nodes part deterministically; taught in Guide Ch 11. **Space colonization** — single-closest-node growth; keep `killRadius` > `stepLength`; sorted Dictionary iteration + a stall counter (`isFinished`). **DLA** — off-lattice walkers; arrival order = index. **Dielectric breakdown** (`DielectricBreakdown`) — NPW lattice model: Laplace solved by incremental Gauss-Seidel (a big settle at init, 2 sweeps riding each step), candidates weighed by `potential^eta` (the *same* read the public `potential(at:)` exposes), 4-neighbor adjacency per the paper; `branches()` decomposes tip-and-fork chain ends so **every edge lands in exactly one channel** (walking only from tips drops the edge above each fork, a caught bug); pipe-model `thicknesses`; `ground: .border` rim vs `.points` electrodes (rim then insulates via mirrored relax); `DielectricBreakdownTests` (7) pins determinism, lattice adjacency, the eta regime, arc-to-ground, edge coverage, field read-back; snapshot `lichtenberg`, example `Patterns/Lichtenberg`, `Docs/Generators/DielectricBreakdown.md`. **Ant colony** (`AntColony`) — Dorigo Ant System: per-ant roulette on `pheromone^alpha · (1/d)^beta`, evaporate-then-deposit with deposits scaled by a greedy-tour yardstick (numbers stay O(1) at any canvas size), `elitism` = the paper's elitist ants; `trails` normalizes to the strongest edge and drops sub-0.5% edges (early frames a full haze, late frames the condensed route); best never worsens (pinned), ring convergence pinned within 2%; `AntColonyTests` (6), snapshot `ant-colony`, example `Patterns/AntColony`, `Docs/Generators/AntColony.md`. **Crack growth** (`CrackGrowth`) — angle-raster subdivision: a cell within 5° reads as the crack's own line, any other angle stops it (restart perpendicular ±2°, recruit one more, capped at `maxCracks`); restarts draw from the kept claimed-cell list, never a bounded random probe (goes dormant on a sparse grid, a real bug the tests caught); `step()` marks carry point + one-sided wash span + random-walk gain, `grains` lays the sin-eased wash (side pinned, verified red by sabotage); `segments` feeds the vector exports; example `Patterns/Cracks` (accumulating, `noClear()`), snapshot `crack-growth`, `CrackGrowthTests`. **Meander** (`Meander`) — curvature-driven channel migration (stateful stepper: `centerline`/`oxbows`/`scars`): per-point drift = local curvature at a negative weight + an exponentially decaying upstream average at a larger positive one (the upstream term dominating is what makes bends deepen *and* slide downstream); the per-step resample is an **interpolating spline, never the straight-segment `resampled(spacing:)`** (a linear resample shaves curvature every step, and curvature is the engine); pinch detection skips `(cutoffDistance + 20·spacing)/spacing` indices along the line so a bend's own flanks never self-cut; the seed line is a few long tapered sine waves, **not white noise** (point-scale jitter carries no energy at the amplified wavelengths and trips the per-step clamp — a measured stall, sinuosity 1.19 vs 1.84 at step 800); oxbows shrink toward their centroid and die under `width/2`; scars trim to `maxScars` in one pass (a one-at-a-time trim never catches up after lowering the ceiling, a caught bug). Example `Patterns/Meander`, snapshot `meander`, `MeanderTests`, `Docs/Generators/Meander.md`. **Strange attractors** — `StrangeAttractor` (RK4) + `ChaoticMap` (clifford/deJong/henon + gumowskiMira/ikeda/hopalong; Gumowski-Mira is the *full* damped form and near-conservative, so the long transient is the picture — plot with no settle, the start picks the structure, and its test pins spread-without-escape, the two degenerate failure modes; ikeda defaults u=0.9, 0.918 parks some starts on the coexisting far sink; hopalong's reach grows with count; GPU mirrors `ollin_gumowski_mira`/`ollin_ikeda`/`ollin_hopalong` in the shader lib; example `Patterns/GumowskiMira`, snapshot `gumowski-mira`); RK4 shared in `Math/Integration.swift`; the GPU leg is `AttractorFlow` (its own bullet above). **Chaotic 1D maps & bifurcation** (`Geometry/Bifurcation.swift`) — `IteratedMap` (logistic/sine/tent/gauss families + custom rule, optional analytic `derivative`): `orbit`/`graph`/`cobweb` + `bifurcation` points / `bifurcationImage` density (ink-on-white, ONE global log factor — the flame rule) / `lyapunovExponent` (exact-value-pinned: ln 2 at logistic r=4, ln r for tent) + `drawBifurcation` sugar; rng-free throughout. **Sweeps sample column centers, never range endpoints** (exactly-r=2 tent collapses to 0 in floating point, one doubled bit at a time) and **factory starts avoid the critical point** (the logistic critical orbit dies in two steps at exactly r=4 — start is 0.3, not 0.5); gauss can hold two attractors at once, so its diagram legitimately depends on `start`. Example `Patterns/Bifurcation` (diagram + Lyapunov trace in a retained batch); snapshot `bifurcation`; `BifurcationTests`. `Docs/Generators/Bifurcation.md`. **Percolation** (`Geometry/Percolation.swift`): site-percolation clusters over a seeded or caller-supplied boolean grid (`criticalProbability` 0.592746 as API): one-pass union-find labeling (4-neighbor only, corner contact never joins), clusters **largest first** (ties by reading order), `spanningClusterIndex` top-to-bottom, `cellRects(of:in:)` placement, and `outlines(of:in:)` closed rectilinear boundary loops (holes included, collinear runs merged, interior on the walker's right, pinch vertices take the tighter turn so loops never self-cross); example `Patterns/Percolation` (fixed per-cell values + breathing threshold, the flood-not-reroll pattern); snapshot `percolation`; `PercolationTests` (10, north-union sabotage-verified red). `Docs/Generators/Percolation.md`. **Fractals** (`Geometry/IFS.swift` / `InversionFractal.swift` / `Kleinian.swift`, `Image/FractalFlame.swift`) — `IFS` (affine `Map`s + weighted chaos game; presets fern/triangle/carpet, the fern grows +y up so examples flip); `FractalFlame` (written from the flame paper ONLY — flam3/Apophysis are GPL, never read; variations use **θ = atan2(x, y)**, the y-axis convention, pinned by `FractalTests` — swapping args is the classic porting bug; ONE log factor from density scales all three channels (independent logs shift hue), gamma at the downfilter; `Renderer` is the progressive form, deterministic per (flame, size, seed, total samples)); `inversionLimitSet` (the canonical walk: start OUTSIDE the discs, random circle but **never the one just used**, no inside/outside test — the pull-inward-only variant collapses tangent arrangements into the tangency points, a real bug); `kleinianLimitSet` (trace recipe on the Markov minus root; the DFS's **cyclic child order + repetend fixed points** are jointly what make the output one ordered closed polyline — change either and the curve disconnects); `Buddhabrot` (`Image/Buddhabrot.swift`, Melinda Green's escaping-orbit density plate: seeds from the **whole upper half of the radius-2 disc** (a smaller box prints its own edge into the density as a rectangle, a real defect); deposits mirrored about the real axis in the upright framing (imaginary across, real down); one cap = gray, three caps = the false-color RGB split, an orbit outliving every cap plots nothing; cardioid + period-2-bulb closed-form interior skips; develop = per-channel **percentile ceiling** + gamma, so hot antenna pixels can't dim the plate; `Renderer` progressive + deterministic like the flame's, slices compose exactly; `BuddhabrotTests` 7, mirror deposit sabotage-verified red); the public **`fitted(_:in:)`** point-cloud placement helper (Rectangle.swift). Examples `Patterns/IteratedFunctions` / `FractalFlame` / `Buddhabrot` / `InversionFractal` / `Kleinian`; snapshots `ifs` / `fractal-flame` / `buddhabrot` / `inversion-fractal` / `kleinian`; `FractalTests`. `Docs/Generators/Fractals.md`. **Schottky circle orbits** (`Geometry/Schottky.swift` + the shared `Geometry/MobiusMap.swift`, the internal `ComplexValue`/`MobiusMap` lifted out of `Kleinian.swift` — the Kleinian walk is byte-identical after the move) — `schottkyCircles` / `schottkyLimitSet` over `SchottkyPairing`, the *circle*-emitting sibling of `kleinianLimitSet`: it renders the disconnected regimes the ordered-curve walk can't, and its `[Circle]` output means SVG/PDF write **true circles** (the plotter path, halftone precedent). **Load-bearing:** a circle's Möbius image is the closed-form **Hermitian** transform `H' = (M⁻¹)†H M⁻¹` (a line image → `nil`, dropped as a branch, measure-zero for circles clear of the pole), and the map must be **unit-determinant** for `inverse` (the adjugate) to be exact; the walk emits `w(C_k)` for every non-cancelling letter and **prunes on that same circle's radius** (the whole subtree nests inside it, so the radius *is* the branch's error bound — no second pass); the pairing's twist zero point folds in **`−u²`** so two *touching* circles paired at `twist: 0` hold their tangency point fixed and the generator is **parabolic**, which is the entire reason a picture stays dense (measured: tangent-pair `schottkyCuspedPairs` 22,660 circles vs cross-ring `schottkyNecklace` 3,208 at its best twist — no twist rescues the necklace), so **`lean` is the animatable dial** (it swings each pair about its own tangency point in opposite senses, keeping both generators parabolic) where **`twist` thins the lace as it grows**; overlapping discs make the group non-discrete and a 2M-circle ceiling ends such a walk rather than exhausting memory. **Viewing, containing, and the trace bridge:** a `viewpoint` re-seats the whole orbit by conjugating with the involution sending that point to the horizon (walk runs in the *viewed* plane so `minRadius` prunes drawn sizes; the disc holding the viewpoint flips to the picture's outer boundary; a viewpoint ON the limit set makes circles above any floor infinite — nudge off it); a pairing disc may be its circle's **exterior** (`fromExterior`/`toExterior`), the containing-circle framing, and the walk's orientation rides the **sign of the Hermitian form's `A'`** (`discImage`), with exterior discs never radius-pruned (their radius bounds nothing — depth stops them); `schottkyCircles(ta:tb:in:)` bridges to the grandma-recipe groups via **isometric circles** as pairing discs (letter k's disc = `gens[k].inverse.isometricCircle`; bases may overlap — nesting is per-map, pruning stays sound; gasket traces `(2,2)` = the Apollonian-gasket packing, the classic figure; grandma's minus-root makes ±imaginary trace perturbations asymmetric — the example ping-pongs the upper arc only). Example `Patterns/Schottky` (declares `loopDuration`); snapshot `schottky`; `SchottkyTests`. **Spanning-tree line art** (`Geometry/SpanningTree.swift`) — `spanningTree(through:/of:)`, the branching sibling of `singleLine`: exact EMST (Kruskal over Delaunay edges, deterministic tie-breaks; collinear input → lexicographic chain, coincident duplicates → nearest-straggler joins), decomposed into the **minimal** open chains — a walk may start only at a *currently*-odd vertex (originally-odd starts strand edges into extra chains, a real bug, pinned by `SpanningTreeTests`). **Isolines** (`Geometry/Isolines.swift`) — `isolines(at:in:resolution:field:)` + levels + image forms, marching squares: **oriented** case table + cell-average saddle rule; **null segments dropped** (a contour through an exact grid corner emits them — two-point litter, a real bug); directional stitch → boundary-exiting contours come back **open**; endpoint maps indexed, never iterated; levels share one sampling; image form = linear-luminance-on-white, levels in **sRGB tone** (the `cutoff` convention). Examples `Images/SpanningTree` + `Patterns/ContourMap`; snapshots `spanning-tree` / `isolines`; `IsolineTests`. `Docs/Generators/SpanningTree.md`, `Docs/Generators/Isolines.md`. **Hulls & medial axis** (`Geometry/ConcaveHull.swift` + `MedialAxis.swift`; `Shape.contains(_:)` added alongside, a winding-honoring ray test) — `concaveHull(of:concavity:)` (chi-shape: erode border triangles longest-boundary-edge-first, blocked when the opposite vertex is already on the boundary; **hull-notch capping is load-bearing** — the substrate's finite super-triangle drops hairline hull slivers, so missing hull edges are capped with sliver fans before erosion, making concavity 0 *exactly* `convexHull` (seed-7 regression in `HullTests`)); `alphaShape(of:alpha:)` (alpha complex, circumradius ≤ alpha; islands + holes as `Shape`s); `medialAxis(of:spacing:prune:)` (Voronoi subcomplex of a boundary resample — non-ring-neighbor + inside filters; radii are exact clearances; **prune collects each round's twigs against the round-start graph and removes them together** — mid-round removal turns a junction into a pass-through and the sibling's walk runs past it, a real bug; a whole-component chain never prunes). All three **canonically order output by geometry, never triangle order** (Bowyer–Watson's Dictionary refan makes triangle *order* process-varying while the *set* is stable — loops start at their lex-min vertex, islands and branches sort by points). Examples `Shapes/Hulls` + `Shapes/MedialAxis`; snapshots `concave-hull` / `medial-axis`; `HullTests` / `MedialAxisTests`. `Docs/Generators/Hulls.md`, `Docs/Generators/MedialAxis.md`. **Straight skeleton** (`Geometry/StraightSkeleton.swift`) — `straightSkeleton(of:)` (exact shrinking-wavefront skeleton: arcs + one face per boundary edge) + `StraightSkeleton.inset(by:)` (mitered insets cut from the faces as roof planes, the contour-ladder tool; `maxInset` the vanish depth; Clipper2 offsets stay the rounded/outward tool). **Load-bearing:** events process in **levels clustered by meeting point / shared parent** and dispatch as chains (the degenerate-pile-up treatment; naive one-at-a-time is famously wrong); events computed during a level that land back on it are **purged as stale echoes**, which is why a 2-vertex LAV closes as a **bridge** (its pair's mutual event always re-lands on the level) and why **coincident LAV neighbors must fuse at level end** (a ghost corner around a zero-length front otherwise starves its LAV — a real bug, 8% of a spiky blob's area vanished, pinned by `facesPartitionSpikyBlobs`); output assembles by **node identity** (each vertex's origin→death arc; faces stitch from their edge's endpoint-role arcs; inset chords match across faces by arc node-pair, no epsilon), and **Σ face areas == shape area** is the test net. Example `Shapes/StraightSkeleton` (island contour ladder); snapshot `straight-skeleton`; `StraightSkeletonTests`. `Docs/Generators/StraightSkeleton.md`. **Painterly marks** (`Geometry/Marbling.swift` + `Watercolor.swift`) — `Marbling` (Jaffer closed-form: area-preserving `drop` to √(d²+r²), the stylus family `tine`/`comb`/`tine(around:)`/`swirl` all displacing `strength · 2^(−d/falloff)` along the tool's motion; rng-free, `add` floats arbitrary shapes holes-intact) + `drawMarbling` (oldest-first fills); **refinement subdivides at *pre-transform* midpoints while the transformed span exceeds `spacing`** (post-hoc refinement can't recover a fold; depth-capped + source-length floor so a drop centered on an outline can't recurse away); rotational tools preserve radius exactly (`MarblingTests`). `Watercolor` (Hobbs recursive midpoint deformation: per-edge variance inherited at 0.4...0.8 with jittered 0.2...1.8 starts; immutable base + further-deformed `layer`s) + `drawWatercolor`; **layers must fill non-zero-wound via `layerShape`/`drawShape`** (they self-cross: even-odd cuts pinholes, and `drawPolygon`'s convex fan shatters them into radial spokes — both real first-render bugs); the **`detail` floor** (edges below it stop subdividing) bounds layer cost regardless of rounds (~40 s → seconds, invisible); painting is setup-shaped (`makeBatch` or `noLoop`). Examples `Patterns/Marbling` + `Shapes/Watercolor`; snapshots `marbling` / `watercolor`; `MarblingTests` / `WatercolorTests`. `Docs/Generators/Marbling.md`, `Docs/Generators/Watercolor.md`. **Chladni figures** (`Math/Chladni.swift`) — `chladni(x,y,m:n:a:b:)`, the center-driven square plate's standing wave in closed form, normalized to −1…1 over plate coordinates 0…1: **`m == n` cancels to zero at the default amplitudes** (1, −1) and the diagonal is always nodal (document, don't "fix"); fractional modes morph figures, so animated tours keep `m > n` at every stop to never cross the degenerate diagonal; mirrored in the shader lib's noise module (`chladni(float2, m, n[, a, b])`) and read by the **`.chladni` Generator** (`ChladniStyle` `.sand` Gaussian gather + hash speckle re-thrown by phase / `.wave` signed swing by `cos(phase)`; `rawIndex` kept in step with `ollin_gen_chladni`). Nodal lines fall out of `isolines(at: 0, ...)` over the field. Examples `Patterns/Chladni` (mode tour) + `Audio/ChladniResonance` (modes picked by *listening*, `magnitude(in:)` per mode pitch); snapshot `chladni`; `ChladniTests`. `Docs/Generators/Chladni.md`. **Terrain** (`Geometry/Heightfield.swift`) — `Heightfield` (closure/`values` inits, bilinear `value(atU:v:)`, `normalized()`, heights 0…1 by convention), `.diamondSquare` (size rounds up to 2^k+1, output normalized, seeded/generic-RNG), `eroded(_:seed:)` over **`Erosion.hydraulic`** (the particle droplet method: inertia-blended bilinear gradient steering, slope·speed·water·capacity carry rule, bilinear-4 deposits, distance-weighted brush erosion; **the speed update is the energy form** `√(max(0, v² − Δh·g))` — the thesis's printed `+Δh·g` slows downhill drops and goes imaginary, a known erratum; heights clamp ≥ 0 so border runoff can't dig unbounded drains; mass only decreases, drops carry sediment off-map) and **`Erosion.thermal`** (talus relaxation, **Jacobi-gathered against the frozen field** so it's order-free, deterministic, exactly mass-conserving; transfer halved so a symmetric pair can't overshoot); `mesh(width:depth:height:)` emits +y-up terrain (CCW-viewed-from-above winding, central-difference normals, plan-view 0…1 UVs — a height-walked `Ramp` texture paints altitude bands) and `image()` the grayscale heightmap. Setup-shaped CPU work (erode once, keep the mesh). Example `3D/Geometry/Terrain` (precomputes raw + eroded meshes for the `weathered` toggle); snapshot `terrain-3d`; `HeightfieldTests`. `Docs/Generators/Terrain.md`. **Isosurfaces & metaballs** (`Geometry/Isosurface.swift` + `Metaballs.swift`) — `isosurface(at:in:resolution:field:) -> Mesh`, marching cubes, deliberately mirroring `isolines`' signature (its 2D sibling); `Metaballs` (`add(at:radius:strength:)` / `value(at:)` / `bounds` / `mesh(resolution:)`) is the headline field. **Load-bearing:** the convention is **inside = field > level** (normals `−∇f`, triangles wound to match, pinned by a positive `signedVolume`), so an SDF must be **negated** to mesh; there is **no 256-case triangulation table** — each cell's surface is built from its **six face contours** (per face: 0/1/2 directed segments with the enclosed region on its left seen from outside, walked CCW) chained into loops and emitted **reversed** (the loop faces inward), and the ambiguous 4-crossing face is settled by the **asymptotic decider** `δ = (a·c − b·d)/(a + c − b − d)` over the face's own four corners, which is *why* it can't crack (both cells read the same four values and the reversed walk swaps starts/ends symmetrically); vertices weld on a **flat lattice-edge array** keyed `(low corner, axis)`, never a Dictionary (determinism), and normals come from the **sampled** gradient (central differences, one-sided at walls) so a smooth field costs no extra field calls; a quad loop splits on its **shorter diagonal**, 5+ fans from an added centroid vertex (cell-interior, so no crack); cells stay **cubic** (`resolution` counts across the longest side) and the grid centers its overshoot; `Metaballs.radius` is the **visible** radius and reach is **2·radius**, because Wyvill's polynomial is exactly 1/2 at u = 1/4 (`falloff` pinned at its three known values), and `mesh()` pads the box by 1.5 cells so the surface closes. Cost is cubic in `resolution` (M2 release, 6 balls at 56: ~2 ms/frame, real-time) and the per-face work is **allocation-free** (flat `faceCorners` + caller-owned scratch, worth ~2× over the obvious per-face arrays); **no UVs** (a blob has no natural parameterization). **Deliberate v1 cut: meshing an `SDF3D` is out** — its evaluation lives only in `ollin_sdf3d_eval`, so a CPU evaluator would be a third hand-synced surface across 15 leaves / 20 combines / 10 transforms. Example `3D/Geometry/Metaballs`; snapshot `metaballs`; `IsosurfaceTests` (watertightness over a saddle-rich field at three resolutions, sphere volume + outward winding, determinism). `Docs/Generators/Isosurface.md`. **Subdivision surfaces** (`Geometry/SubdivisionSurface.swift`) — `mesh.subdivided(_:levels:)` refines any `Mesh` as a control cage: `.catmullClark` (default, general polygon faces) / `.loop` (triangle rules with Warren's β, plus a final **limit push** — γ = 1/(n + 3/(8β)) interior, (3v+b₁+b₂)/5 on rims). **Load-bearing:** the positional **weld tolerance is ~1e-6 of the mesh extent** — libtess2 caps come back Float-quantized (~1e-8 relative) against exact-double walls, so a tighter weld leaves every cap rim open (a real bug); **quad recovery** pairs the consecutive `(a,b,c),(a,c,d)` triangle pattern `gridQuad` emits (run *before* any winding flip — flipping first breaks the pattern); the **coplanar face merge** (CC only) re-derives whole polygon faces for exactly-flat patches (single simple boundary loop, no interior vertices — a deliberately tessellated flat grid is untouched) because a triangulator's arbitrary cap diagonals give identical corners different valences and an extruded star smooths visibly lopsided (0.2 arm spread → 1.7e-8, the five-fold test); **orientation follows the input's normals, not its winding** — `Mesh.extrude` winds inward while shading outward (a find worth knowing), so agreement is area-weighted-summed and a disagreeing mesh's faces flip; no normals → authored winding kept; open/non-manifold edges take the B-spline crease rules, a boundary vertex with one incident face **pins** (the edge-and-corner convention, so open sheets keep their extent), flat sheets stay exactly flat; deterministic (dictionaries lookup-only, first-come weld ids); 2M-face cap with a printed note; output = smooth area-weighted normals, **no uvs**, material carried. Example `3D/Geometry/SubdivisionSurfaces`; snapshot `subdivision-surfaces`; `SubdivisionSurfaceTests` (hand-derived mask pins — cube corner 5/18 + edge 3/8, the Loop strip's odd/crease points — plus watertightness, Euler, convex-hull containment). Taught in Guide Ch 21. `Docs/Generators/SubdivisionSurfaces.md`. **Mesh growth** (`Geometry/MeshGrowth.swift` + `MeshOperators.swift` + `MeshReactionDiffusion.swift`) — `MeshGrowth`, a stateful stepper that grows a `Mesh` surface until it folds, over four `GrowthDriver`s (`.uniform` / `.curvature` / `.field(closure)` / `.chemical(SurfaceChemistry)`); the public `MeshReactionDiffusion` runs the same Gray-Scott on a *fixed* mesh (Turk's surface-texture use, `displaced(by:)` to see it). **Load-bearing:** forces run **along edges only**, which lie in the surface, so nothing can inflate the form and *every* driver folds — the driver picks **where and at what scale**, not whether (documenting `.uniform` as "inflates smoothly" was wrong, caught by render, and the buckling is correct); **self-avoidance excludes the 2-hop neighborhood, not just the 1-ring** (a 2-ring vertex sits ~2·`edgeLength` off on any smooth surface, so leaving it in makes every vertex shove its own neighborhood and the whole form inflates with growth *off* — the defect `aZeroFieldDoesNotGrow` pins), radius default 2·`edgeLength`, past ~3· it inflates again; **`stiffness` is the fold-size dial** (the normal half of the relaxation Laplacian — a sheet with no bending resistance buckles at the triangle scale and reads as crumpled paper, which is the thin-shell term the reference has and the first cut omitted); remeshing is **Botsch-Kobbelt isotropic** (split >4/3·L, collapse <4/5·L guarded by the **link condition** + both rings' resulting lengths + no normal flip, flip toward valence 6, tangential relax at 1/5) and **growth must stop at `maxVertices`** or the springs stretch triangles the splitter can no longer subdivide; per-vertex `channels` (the chemicals) interpolate at a split and average at a collapse so a pattern survives the topology churning; the boundary is **never collapsed or flipped** and a rim vertex relaxes *along* the rim, so an open sheet keeps its edge. **Both types weld first via the shared `WeldedMesh`** — Ollin's generators emit flat-shaded meshes whose triangles share no vertices (an icosphere is degree-2 everywhere), so an unwelded surface cannot diffuse at all and the reaction silently dies; `MeshReactionDiffusion` simulates on the welded copy and republishes through `remap` so `values` still matches `mesh.positions` by index. The **cotangent Laplace-Beltrami operator with mixed areas** (Meyer et al.) is the shared substrate (`MeshOperators`, internal): scaled by mean-edge-length² it is dimensionless and lands at **exactly 4 on a regular triangulation, matching a grid's 5-point stencil**, which is *why* the classic Gray-Scott constants (0.16 / 0.08, feed/kill matching `Sim.reactionDiffusion`) transfer unchanged; a seed patch is **u=0.5, v=0.25 floored at 3·edgeLength across** (pure reagent has nothing to react with and decays; a patch a vertex or two wide diffuses away before igniting). **`settleSteps`** (default 0, `.chemical` only) runs the opening chemistry all at once at the first step so growth answers a formed pattern — and it **must refine the surface to the target edge length first**: chemistry settled on the coarse seed cage dies at exactly zero (the patch is a vertex or two wide there, the ignition floor above — a probe-caught bug); it's the *ramp* fix only, since the pattern covers part of the surface and chemical growth stays a few times slower than `.uniform` at equal `growthAmount` (rate compensation stays with the sketch). Determinism: edges come from a **sorted key array**, never a Dictionary walk, and the repulsion hash grid dedupes its 27 slot reads (two cells can share a slot and would otherwise double-count). Cost is real CPU mesh processing (~30 ms/step at 9k vertices on M2 after a 3× pass: ring table built once, flat edge/boundary table, counting-sort grid, allocation-free `EdgeFaces`), so `maxVertices` is the interactivity dial. Example `3D/Geometry/MeshGrowth`; snapshot `mesh-growth`; `MeshGrowthTests`. `Docs/Generators/MeshGrowth.md`. **Point-cloud surfacing** (`Geometry/ParticleSurface.swift` + `SurfaceReconstruction.swift` over the internal `PointGrid3` + `partialIsosurface`) — `particleSurface(of:radius:blend:resolution:)` (the blended union-of-balls skin: field = radius − |p − kernel-weighted local average|, so an isolated point is an exact ball; no normals, always closes) and `reconstructSurface(of:spacing:resolution:orientedToward:maxGap:neighbors:fitting:keepingLargestComponent:)` (the faithful path: PCA tangent planes over k-NN, signed distance to the nearest plane, marched by the internal **`partialIsosurface`**, whose nil-mask path is byte-identical to `isosurface`). The **`fitting:` choice** (`SurfaceFitting`, an artwork parameter per the catalog rule, never a `RenderQuality` tier): `.planes` default (byte-identical to the shipped path — pinned), `.robust(sharpness:iterations:)` = **RIMLS** (Öztireli/Guennebaud/Gross 2009, written from the paper — MeshLab's implementation is GPL, never read): IMLS base (kernel-weighted average of plane heights, kernel `(1−d²/h²)⁴`, per-point h = reach clamped to [1.5, 4]·spacing) iteratively re-weighted by a residual Gaussian (Welsch, σ_r 0.5 **in units of h**) × a normal-vs-gradient Gaussian (σ_n = 1/sharpness floored at 0.5, where the surface genuinely disconnects); **the first pass is unweighted on purpose** (a robust start breaks the reconstruction's continuity — the paper's deliberate deviation from textbook IRLS, so `iterations: 1` is plain smooth IMLS) and **the gradient keeps the kernel-derivative terms** (`(Σαφ′f − f·Σαφ′ + Σαφn)/Σαφ` — the weighted normal average alone is the classic shortcut bug); both fittings share **one plane-based validity gate**, so holes open identically; the evaluator reuses scratch buffers across the serial march (per-lattice allocation is the debug-build trap) with flat scalar inner loops. Measured on the staged partial-coverage ball (the phantom-fringe scene): mid-air phantom vertices 5,480 → 43, ball-annulus 1,747 → 0, the ball's observed front kept (3,169 vs 3,210 on-ball vertices) — so the fringe envelope below is now `.planes`-specific. **Colors carry:** the `PointCloud` overload transfers the cloud's colors (nearest sample, `Mesh.colored(from:)`), skipping all-white clouds so bare reconstructions stay colorless; `largestComponent` remaps colors with positions. **Load-bearing:** orientation is **viewpoints-first**, by a **distance-weighted vote (1/d⁴) over the (nearest ≤16) viewpoints — never the nearest alone** (the nearest camera sees a terminator sample edge-on, dot ≈ 0, sign = noise → flipped planes raise phantom curtains, probe-confirmed; the 1/d⁴ falloff keeps thin walls decided by proximity); the spanning-tree fallback weights edges 1−|nᵢ·nⱼ|, which reads antiparallel as agreement, so it **can walk through thin double-sided structure and flip a region** (documented limit, not tunable); the field is **undefined, never guessed, where the projected foot lands beyond the validity cutoff** (nil = per-point k-th-neighbor reach **capped at 3.5·spacing** — uncapped, the reach *inflates* exactly where data is missing and phantom bowls grow in occlusion shadows; tighter caps pinhole randomly-sampled clouds, whose local gaps routinely pass 2.5·spacing; `.infinity` closes everything), which is what keeps scan holes open — and cells touching an undefined sample are skipped whole; **partial coverage frays by design under `.planes`**: an open data edge grows a ~gap-wide fringe along its tangent planes just past the samples (four knob "fixes" probed and refuted — coverage is the fix, so sweep around bodies; `.robust` suppresses >99% of the fringe, measured, but coverage stays the honest fix; the Docs page says both), and **the marching cell must stay under ~2·spacing** (a wide-bounds cloud starves the grid and junctions shred — printed note; crop to the region of interest); the **near-surface occupancy band** short-circuits far cells in one array read but **must not run under `.infinity`** (closing legitimately caps far from samples); `PointGrid3` ring searches **stop on a strict bound only** (in-box `ring·cell`, clamped `(ring−1)·cell`; a tie at the bound must expand another ring — the stipple stopping rule in 3D) and its queries are **flat scalar loops on purpose** (per-candidate closures + `Vector3` operators ran 8.7× slower in the unoptimized builds examples run under); counting-sort buckets ascend by index → byte-reproducible output. Example `3D/Geometry/SurfaceFromPoints`; snapshot `surface-from-points`; `SurfaceReconstructionTests` (grid matched against brute force, watertight sphere volume + outward wind, torus Euler 0, a hole stays open, thin-wall viewpoint orientation, byte-determinism both paths). `Docs/Generators/SurfaceReconstruction.md`. **Articulated & chaotic motion** — `IKChain` (`maxBend` enforced *inside* both FABRIK passes then re-placed at exact segment length, never post-clamped; keep the stall exit), `DoublePendulum` (fixed ≤1/480 s substeps — reproducible by default), `NBody` (Barnes-Hut; coincident bodies chain at the depth cap; θ=0 stays the exact all-pairs sum — don't "tighten" `NBodyTests`' error bound). `Docs/Simulation/Motion.md`. **`Color`** — CSS Color 4 named set + `Color(hex:)`, HSB (hue wraps), the OKLab family (`OKLab` / `OKLCH` / `OKHSL`, gamut-mapped, written from Ottosson), `Color.mix(_:_:t:in:)`, `Color(kelvin:)`, `withAlpha(_:)` / perceptual `luminance`, `Ramp` / `Palette` (ColorBrewer + harmonies), `CosinePalette` (iq), `Colormap`. **Palette import** — hex / CSV / TSV / JSON / ASE; the three text formats share **one parser** and `.auto` sniffs palette-per-line from the *file's* shape, an explicit format **overrides** the sniff (`palettePerLine: Bool?`, nil = decide); **no comment syntax is promised** (`#deface` is a color); loaders never trap on malformed bytes; **ship the loader, not the data** (bundled palette data stays license-gated). **Palette extraction** — weighted k-means++ → Lloyd in **OKLab**; identical pixels merged and **sorted before clustering**; output most-used first; deterministic given (image, count, seed). **Image dithering** — `Image.dithered`: 8 error-diffusion kernels + centered Bayer + generated `.blueNoise`; CPU-only by nature; **three color spaces, one per family — mixing them is the bug**: tone accumulates in **linear**, `.none` picks **OKLab**-nearest, error diffusion picks ***linear***-nearest (the false-contour rule, pinned by `DitherTests` on a 1×1 image); threshold maps run the **Yliluoma pair search** (variance-weighted penalty, linear mix fraction, dark→light phase); a fully transparent pixel diffuses no residual. **Print separations** — `Image.separated(into:paper:)` under the translucent-spot-ink overprint model (linear-light mixing, OKLab-judged coverage search); screening drops coverage under the **2% minimum dot**; the sketch declares **`printInks`** (NOT `inks`); `--export-separations` (registration band around the untouched artwork; recipes carry `ExportMetadata.inks`). `Docs/Output/PrintSeparations.md`. **Gradient paint** — `Gradient` (`.linear` / `.radial` / `.alongPath`) / `Paint` on every shape via a baked 256-texel LUT strip (SDF path) + per-vertex evaluation (tessellated path), with native SVG gradient export; shared CPU↔GPU structs live once in the C header (see *Shaders*). `Docs/Drawing/Geometry.md`, `Docs/Drawing/Color.md`, `Docs/Drawing/Epicycles.md`, `Docs/Drawing/Voronoi.md`, `Docs/Drawing/Truchet.md`, `Docs/Drawing/Attractors.md`, and the per-generator `Docs/Generators/*` pages.

- **3D (camera, depth, point clouds, meshes).** Opt-in: `camera(_:)` / `perspective(...)` / `ortho(...)` over `Camera3D` (per-frame state) allocates a camera-gated `depth32Float` buffer (a 2D-only frame allocates none and renders byte-identically). Right-handed, y-up; 3D rides the camera, not the 2D CTM. **Point clouds** (`PointCloud` / `drawPointCloud`, instanced camera-facing splats). **4×4 transform stack** (`translate(x,y,z)` / `rotateX/Y/Z` / `rotate(_:axis:)` / `scale` / `transform(_ matrix:)`, the last composing a whole `simd_float4x4` in one call for transforms that arrive whole — a streamed body anchor, a joint pose — verified pixel-identical to the equivalent TRS calls; a separate stack from the 2D CTM, bakes into geometry CPU-side). **Solid primitives** — the public `Mesh` value type + a broad generator catalog (`drawBox` / `drawSphere` / `drawIcosphere` / `drawCylinder` / `drawCone` / `drawCapsule` (+ `drawCapsule(from:to:radius:)`, spanning two 3D points end to end with its round tips on the points — the solid bone/strut) / `drawTorus` / `drawTorusKnot` / `drawPyramid` / `drawHelix` / the Platonic set / `drawMobius` / `drawKlein` / `drawSuperellipsoid` / `drawSupershape` / `drawExtrude` / `drawLathe` / `drawTube`) on a depth-tested mesh pipeline. **Generator meshes are consistently wound outward, and their normals agree with that winding** — load-bearing because nothing culls back faces and every mesh carries its own normals, so a badly wound surface still *draws* and the defect hides, while everything reading a mesh as a connected solid (a soft body's springs and its inside, a subdivision cage's orientation, a traced ray's facing) reads the winding: `Mesh.cylinder` wound both cap fans into the solid (which detonated a soft cylinder in half a second), `pyramid`/`roundedBox`/`extrude`/`cone` carried versions of the same, and `parametricSurface` derived `du × dv` against quads wound the other way (shading superellipsoid/supershape/mobius/klein from the unlit side). `MeshTests` pins both invariants across the catalog; a supershape's ring beside each pinch is the one allowed exception (its central differences measure nothing there). **Per-vertex mesh colors** — `Mesh.colors` (aligned with `positions`, the `uvs` rule) **multiply** the resolved surface color at the `drawMesh` vertex bake (fill × material baseColor × vertex color, the texture contract — white fill shows a scan's colors untouched, fill stays a whole-mesh tint); an empty `colors` takes the constant-color path byte-identically, a wireframe ignores them (edges are the stroke), no shader/stride change (the mesh vertex already carried per-vertex color); painted via `Mesh.colored(by:)` (position+normal closure) / `colored(from: PointCloud)` (nearest sample over `PointGrid3`), and **read from file** — glTF `COLOR_0` (VEC3/VEC4 × float / normalized u8 / normalized u16, **re-encoded linear→sRGB** like every glTF factor) + Model I/O's `MDLVertexAttributeColor` (PLY per-vertex color, already *display* values so **no** re-encode; the two conventions differ and mixing them is the bug); a primitive/mesh without colors **fills white** (the multiply identity, so a partial set is lossless) and colors survive if *any* did, unlike the all-or-nothing `uvs` rule, while a file with none yields **empty** `colors` (the constant-color path stays byte-identical). **Model I/O gotcha:** `vertexAttributeData(forAttributeNamed:as:)` returns *garbage* (the first vertex repeated) when the requested format changes the component count and never returns nil, so read the descriptor's declared format and match its width (low byte of `MDLVertexFormat.rawValue`). **`subdivided(_:)` carries colors**, refined by the same masks the positions take (mirrored *beside* the position math, never folded into it — the position path is byte-pinned by the snapshot + the five-fold-symmetry test; the weld keeps the first-claiming vertex's color, and Loop's limit push applies to colors too). `MeshColorTests` (transforms + Metal-gated multiply/variation probes) + `MeshLoaderTests` + `SubdivisionSurfaceTests`. **Lights & materials** — `directionalLight` / `pointLight` / `spotLight` / `ambientLight` + `Light`, Blinn-Phong, **auto-lit by default** (`.auto` / `.custom` / `.off`, `lights()` / `noLights()`); curated `lightingPreset(_:)` / `LightingPreset`; **area lights** — `rectLight` / `diskLight` / `tubeLight` (`Light.rect/.disk/.tube`, kinds 3-5): linearly-transformed-cosine shading (rect = the exact horizon-clipped polygon integral, disk = the ellipse eigen + cubic + tabulated clipped-sphere path, tube = the analytic line integral × radius, capless) through the **bundled fitted tables** (`Resources/LTC/`, BSD-3 + notice, float32 from the reference `ltc.js`) at fragment **textures 8/9 on every `meshLitColor` carrier** (solid / textured / raymarch / half-res tier), gated by `OllinLighting.ltcEnabled` → **byte-identical with no area light**; `intensity` × `color` = the surface's **radiance** (physical falloff — the punctual kinds keep no-attenuation); **load-bearing:** the packed frame is right-handed (`tangent × bitangent = facing normal` — the shader's corner winding and disk front-test depend on it), the cubic/eigen **sqrt args clamp at 0** (float dip under exact-math-nonnegative → NaN black speckle where a surface grazes the disk's plane, a real fixed bug), non-PBR materials map `shininess` → perceptual roughness `(2/(s+2))^(1/4)` with specular = norm channel × `mat.specular` (inert at 0, the finish rule), the RT-reflection hit shade evaluates the **exact LTC diffuse** per panel (`ollin_ltc_diffuse`, kept in step with `ollin_ltc_light`'s dispatch; only the disk reads a table, so the one amp texture rides the trace — bound at texture 9 of the deferred pass, whose lighting must also resolve `ltcEnabled` or panels go dark only in deferred reflections; hit specular stays the traced second bounce, matching the punctual Lambert-only treatment; snapshot `area-reflections` (RT-gated) + `AreaReflectionRenderProbes`), and the SSS back term is the **flipped-normal integral** (carries the panel's falloff). **Area (rect/disk) cast shadows** — the caster search appends rect/disk **after** the punctual kinds (existing scenes keep their caster; tubes never cast — radial emission has no facing axis): the penumbra comes from the panel's **real extent** (RT device: rays to deterministic points on the actual panel surface — Vogel in a disk's plane, antithetic R2 pairs on a rect; else a spot-style map **aimed at the camera target**, PCSS radius = half-extent in texels capped at 40), `shadowSoftness` *scales* that extent (0 hard / 0.5 default = physical / 1 = 2×), the RT flip **overwrites `shadowDepthB`** with the softness scale (the map path needs the slot for the perspective linearization), and the shadow dims **both LTC integrals** inside the area branch (atten on `diffI`/`specI` → every model + SSS dim consistently; no caster multiplies by exactly 1.0, byte-identical). Examples `3D/Lighting/AreaLights` + `AreaShadows`; snapshots `area-lights` + `area-shadows` (RT-gated); `LightingTests` packing + `AreaLightRenderProbes` (one-sided / two-sided / falloff) + `AreaShadowRenderProbes` (on/off diff isolates the shadow; path-agnostic, so they cover the map fallback too). Mechanism: ARCHITECTURE.md § *Area lights* + § *Shadows*. **Light shaping (IES profiles + cookies)** — `IESProfile` (LM-63 Type C parser: tokenize *all* numbers after the `TILT=` line, real files break the line rules; lateral symmetry keyed to the last horizontal angle 0/90/180/360 + the rare 90→270 case; candela blocks per horizontal angle with vertical fastest; normalized to peak 1; `init?` never traps — header `Int(Double)` overflow guarded) rides point + spot via `profile:` (`axis:` aims a point light, default straight down; `roll:` spins profile azimuth + cookie together), and **`LightCookie`** rides spots (resamples an `Image` *once* into its own fixed 512² buffer at init — it exists because `Light` is Equatable/Sendable and `Image` is neither; GPU-backed images fail to wrap). GPU: profiles bake 256×64 `r16Float` layers and cookies sRGB layers into two `texture2d_array`s at fragment **textures 10/11 on every carrier** (solid / textured / raymarch / half-res + the deferred RT-reflect trace), content-hash-cached with a *fresh* texture on change (never replaced in place), stand-in a 1×1 *array* (the 2D `strip` is type-invalid for an array slot); `iesEnabled` / `cookieEnabled` copy the `ltcEnabled` discipline (the same three set-sites, deferred pass included — same pattern-lost-only-in-reflections gotcha) → **byte-identical featureless frames** (whole suite un-recorded); layer indices ride the new `OllinLight.shaping` float4, deduped by `makeLighting` into `Drawer.usedIESProfiles`/`usedLightCookies` (rebuilt every call, so repeated per-frame calls agree). Shaping applies on the light's **local copy** (`L.color/.specular *=` tint → every shading model and `ollin_rt_direct` pick it up); `ollin_light_frame` is the **projector convention** (`right = axis × ref` — the cross(ref, axis) order mirrors the projected image, a caught bug); cookie edges land at the outer cone (the [0,1]² square contains the cone circle, so the edge clamp is invisible); a profile is dark outside its measured range, so a 90°-capped file sends nothing above the fixture's horizon — a wallwasher *tilts its axis* at the wall (the example's lesson). Example `3D/Lighting/LightShaping` (authored `.ies` resources, never manufacturer data); snapshot `light-shaping`; `IESProfileTests` (parse / symmetry folds / ordering / bake / packing) + `LightShapingRenderProbes` (ring profile / cookie orientation / roll). **Atmosphere (fog + aerial perspective + volumetric light)** — `fog(_:density:heightFalloff:)` / `noFog()` (exact closed-form distance/height transmittance per fragment) + `aerialPerspective(density:haziness:heightFalloff:sun:)` / `noAerialPerspective()` (the Hoffman-Preetham wavelength split: per-channel extinction over the same optical depth + closed-form sun in-scatter, Rayleigh + HG phases) + `volumetricLight(_:anisotropy:)` / `noVolumetricLight()` (the Toth-Umenhoffer single-scatter march: directional + spot beams shaped by cone/IES/cookie, shafts carved by the 2D-map caster's shadow) + `volumetricQuality`/`volumetricSteps` (the fourth `RenderQuality` knob, 16/32/64 by tier, both lighting-resolution sites). **Load-bearing:** the constants ride three `OllinLighting` tail fields gated by `fogColor.w` (0 → byte-identical, suite-verified) and pack **before** the `.off` early return (unlit scenes fog); surfaces fog **themselves** to their own depth and the air is a fullscreen skybox-slot draw (`.fogAir`, premultiplied `(inscatter, 1−T)`) so call-ordered compositing needs no stored scene depth (splats/particles composite over the beam-painted backdrop, 2D never fogs, a batch-less frame skips the air draw); the march's **ray∩cone span bounding** (thin beams otherwise dissolve into a woven lattice or vanish un-jittered), **light-leg extinction** (the integral is otherwise unbounded riding a cone; beams-only mode substitutes a 0.05 reference density for σs and that leg), and **per-step re-read jitter** (a single shared offset reprints the jitter pattern as a lattice) are each a fixed real defect — don't simplify any away; jitter is a pure function of (pixel, step) so exports reproduce with no temporal history; the RT hit shade applies the analytic leg **inside the shared trace** (inline + deferred agree; env-miss sky stays clear, documented). **Aerial invariants:** it is **mode 2 on the fog gate** (`fogColor.w` 0/1/2, so every existing `> 0` gate stays armed and one compare picks the model) with two appended `OllinLighting` tail fields; **`ollin_apply_fog` stays verbatim and aerial is a twin beside it** (`ollin_apply_aerial` — the GI fast-math codegen rule; classic fog pinned pixel-identical by a clean-HEAD-worktree double record of all 182 refs); `OLLIN_AERIAL_RAYLEIGH` mirrors `Drawer.aerialRayleighRatios` **by hand** (the `ollin_sdf_distance` rule); the sun resolves explicit → the `.sky` env through its rotation (world sun = R_y(−rotation)·(0, sin e, cos e), sign confirmed against the drawn disc) → first directional light negated → a default elevation, and its radiance is airmass-reddened white (a horizon sun feeds the veil sunset color, no knob); a **nil density derives as 0.35/eye-to-target** (the contact-shadow default rule); **the air veil steps aside behind a skybox** (renderer-set `fogParams2.z`; the sky already is this scattering at infinity — beams still add), and with no backdrop the air paints the implied horizon glow; long paths **saturate toward the sky tone by design** (the closed form's equilibrium — a milky frame means density too high for the framing, not a defect). Examples `3D/Effects/Fog` + `3D/Effects/AerialPerspective` + `3D/Lighting/VolumetricLight`; snapshots `fog` + `volumetric-light` + `aerial-perspective`; `VolumetricTests` (packing + the ordering/shaft/beams-only probes) + `AerialPerspectiveTests` (packing/sun-resolution/reset + the blue-veil/sunward/haziness/sky-byte-hold probes, sabotage-verified). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Atmosphere*. `Docs/3D/Atmosphere.md`. the stylized **`material(_:)` / `Material`** library (toon / gooch + specular / Fresnel-rim / subsurface / iridescent / sparkle-flake finishes over a per-batch uniform; sparkle cells sized by `OllinLighting.sceneScale` so the default works at any scene scale; **soap-film mode** — `iridescenceFlow`/`iridescencePhase`/`iridescenceFlowSize` turn the iridescent rim sheen into a thickness-driven film: drainage via the normal's y (quadratic, so interference bands crowd toward the bottom) + a **domain-warped 3D-fbm swirl** (fbm displaced by a vector of two more fbm reads — single-octave noise only makes blobs, the warp+octaves make the sheared marble wisps; `fbm(float3)` added to OllinShaderLib mirroring the CPU name) in sceneScale-sized cells scaled by `flowSize` (the sparkle-size rule — a small bubble under a far camera needs ~0.3-0.45 or the swirl is near-constant across it), colored by per-RGB-wavelength two-beam interference (rates 1/1.2146/1.4513 — zero thickness goes dark, the black-film cap; a `exp(-0.18·d)` broadband rolloff washes thick film pale), phase is the sketch-driven clock (no hidden time), flow 0 keeps the plain-sheen expression **verbatim** (byte-identical, suite-verified); example `3D/Materials/SoapBubble` composes it on thin `.glass()` — deliberately env-refraction only, since overlapping bubbles would hit the glass-through-glass opaque envelope under RT; snapshot `soap-film`); the **physically-based metallic-roughness finish** (`Material.physicallyBased` + `.metal(roughness:)` / `.dielectric(roughness:)` + the brushed/polished/plastic built-ins) — Cook-Torrance GGX (glTF 2.0 / Filament forms, reimplemented from the math), surface color stays `fill`; it's `shadingModel 3`, so every non-PBR material stays **byte-identical**. **Glass (transmission & refraction)** — `Material.glass(roughness:ior:thickness:attenuationColor:attenuationDistance:)` / `.frostedGlass`: the KHR-transmission/volume/ior model riding the PBR finish (no new shading model), the transmitted lobe **replacing the diffuse one** (`mix` by `transmission·(1−metallic)` in the IBL ambient; punctual + LTC diffuse scale by the same `diffKeep`). **Load-bearing:** F0 now comes CPU-packed as `mat.f0`, the **exact literal 0.04 at ior 1.5** (the computed form rounds to a different float — `MaterialTests` pins it) so pre-glass frames stay bit-identical; **transmission is inert without an environment** (nothing to transmit → shades as the plain dielectric, probe-pinned byte-equal) and inert at 0; the base path is env refraction (analytic interior span, curvature-blended exit normal, blur lod fading as ior→1, Beer-Lambert `pow(att.rgb, span/att.w)` with channels **floored 1e-4** — a zero channel NaNs `pow(0,0)` under fast math); `rayTracedReflections()` upgrades glass too (one opt-in for mirrors + glass): the traced walk reads **raw hit facing** — a back face is the solid's real exit (refract out, trace on, traced span feeds Beer-Lambert), a front face inside is an embedded object, a thin wall hops its own shell's back faces (≤4), TIR carries straight on — and hits shade via `ollin_rt_hit_radiance`, **extracted from the reflection trace so mirror and glass can't drift**; refraction traces **inline even in deferred frames** (refracted content is minified; revisit on shimmer). v1 envelope: opaque shadows, opaque seen in mirrors / through other glass. Example `3D/Materials/Glass`; snapshots `glass-materials` + `rt-refraction-3d` (RT-gated); `GlassRenderProbes` (env-inert byte-equality / absorption-vs-span / traced-wall-through-pane) + `MaterialTests` packing. Mechanism: ARCHITECTURE.md § *Glass: transmission and refraction*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#glass`. **Anisotropic specular (the brushed streak)** — `Material.anisotropy` (−1…1) + `anisotropyRotation` (radians, packed as cos/sin CPU-side): the Kulla at/ab split (at = α(1+a), ab = α(1−a)) over the anisotropic GGX distribution + height-correlated anisotropic Smith visibility on the punctual lobe in *both* lit twins, and the bent-reflection-vector environment gather (RT reflections trace the bent direction for free; coat + sheen keep the plain mirror direction `Rmirror`; LTC area lights stay isotropic, the fitted tables having no anisotropic form). **The isotropic helpers stay the strength-0 path** (the gate keeps every non-anisotropic frame byte-identical, suite-verified). The tangent frame is the real per-vertex basis on the nm/surface-mapped pipelines (orthonormalized against the shading normal), else the stable world fallback (`ollin_ltc_any_tangent`), which reads as a lathe finish on primitives (poles pinch by design); `.brushedMetal` carries the finish. Example `3D/Materials/BrushedMetal`; snapshot `anisotropy-materials`; `AnisotropyPackingTests` + `AnisotropyRenderProbes` (streak-extent probes: extents and peaks, not region means). `Docs/3D/3D.md#anisotropy`. **Clearcoat & sheen (layered PBR lobes)** — `clearcoat`/`clearcoatRoughness` + `sheen`/`sheenColor`/`sheenRoughness` on the PBR finish (presets `.carPaint(roughness:)` / `.lacquer` / `.satin` / `.felt`): the coat is a second GGX lobe (Kelemen visibility, fixed 0.04 Schlick) with the base dimmed by (1−Fc) and its F0 remapped for the coat interface — **the remap sends the default 0.04 to exactly 0** (the interface vanishes), so a coated base legitimately loses its broad spec and a probe must compare **peaks, not region means** (the first probe draft failed on this); sheen is the Charlie sine NDF + cloth visibility, no Fresnel, base scaled by 1−max(tint)·E. **Load-bearing:** E is the **sheen LUT** (`ollin_ibl_sheen_lut`, r16Float 64², baked lazily by `ensureSheenLUT` beside the three `resolveIBL` call sites when a frame's batches carry sheen — environment-independent but needed under plain lights, so it triggers off materials, not the env bake; **fragment texture 12 on every `meshLitColor` carrier**, stand-in otherwise); both lobes branch on their packed fields → **byte-identical at zero** (suite-verified, no re-records); under LTC area lights the coat runs a **second LTC fetch at the coat roughness** and sheen takes **E·diffI** (the broad-lobe treatment); under RT reflections the coat **reuses the traced radiance** (same mirror direction, no second trace) while sheen keeps the prefiltered env; a surface seen *in* a mirror shades without the lobes (the glass-in-glass envelope); `OllinMaterial` grew 176→192 (`clearcoat` fills the old offset-124 hole, `clearcoatRoughness` the 172 tail pad, `sheenColor` appended, strength premultiplied into its rgb with roughness in w). Example `3D/Materials/CoatAndCloth`; snapshot `coat-sheen`; `CoatSheenRenderProbes` (point / panel / env coat peaks + the sheen rim) + `MaterialTests`. Mechanism: ARCHITECTURE.md § *Clearcoat and sheen*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#clearcoat-sheen`. **Real subsurface scattering (the separable diffusion)** — `Material.scattering` (0…1) / `scatteringRadius` (**world units**, the one scene-dependent number) / `scatteringColor` (per-channel falloff ratios, default the warm skin set) + `.skin(radius:)` / `.marble(radius:)`: a screen-space diffusion, two `ollin_sss_blur` passes over the linear pre-tonemap resolve inserted between the geometry resolve and the frame filters on every render path (live / headless / benchmark; accumulation + texture-handoff keep their no-deferred-pass envelope), fed by a dedicated **scatter-mask** mesh re-encode with its own depth (occluders suppress hidden skin; matcaps occlude but never scatter). **Load-bearing:** the mask pipeline (`isScatterMask`) runs **blending off** — its alpha carries the *profile index*, which `.normal` blending would multiply into the color; the kernel is **CPU-built and pure** (25 importance-placed taps over the published sum-of-Gaussians skin profile stretched per channel by the falloff, normalized then strength-folded into the center tap, so strength 0 is *exactly* the identity; cached by profile, ≤8 distinct profiles/frame, extras reuse the last + note); the **depth-gap guard measures against the scattering radius** (tap fully cut at four radii, un-projecting the mask's step via `2·step·wFactor/P11`) — never a fixed screen-space constant, which is proportional to radius and saturates on the surface's own curvature the moment the radius is a visible fraction of the object, silently disabling the whole blur (a real first-render bug); unmarked pixels pass through **bit-exact** and a no-scattering frame skips the stage byte-identical (whole suite unchanged, no re-records); `OllinMaterial` grew 192→224 (`scatter` float4: raw falloff ratios floored 0.001 + radius in w, `scatterStrength` + explicit pads); the lit fragments read those fields only inside the transmittance branch (gated on `scatterStrength > 0`), so every non-scattering shading path is untouched. **Transmittance (the translucency half)** — under a `castShadows()` caster the same material transmits through thin backlit geometry: thickness from the caster's own depth (2D map via the new `OllinLighting.shadowLinearize` [2][2]/[3][2] constants — the PCSS ratio needs only [2][2], an absolute distance both — with the receiver shrunk 2 map texels along its normal, each tap a **per-corner-linearized manual bilinear** read (blending perspective depths first bends the ramp; a nearest tap terraces), **gathered over the diffusion's entry footprint** (`transmitGather2D`: a world-anchored-rotated 13-tap spiral sized by the scattering radius in world units — never map texels — per-Gaussian-normalized so a constant-thickness slab reduces exactly to the slab form; a single-tap read re-exposes the caster mesh's own tessellation at grazing light, the audit's terminator stripes, and the fix is red-first-pinned by `grazingLightLeavesTheTerminatorSmooth`); cube via its stored linear distance; RT point via one closest-hit ray, `meshRTThickness`, traced by the solid/textured fragments under the same material gate); `ollin_sss_transmit` is the **closed-form slab integral of the same Gaussian table `scatterKernel` uses** (w·e^(−s²/2v), hand-synced — the `ollin_sdf_distance` rule), thickness entering in kernel units (×3/radius); irradiance is the reversed-normal 0.3-wrap. **The term reads the pre-shadow attenuation** (a backlit face sits in its own body's shadow — dimming by it erases exactly the transported light) while riding the cone gate + shaped light copy, and lands before the blur (diffused with the reflectance, the published treatment); **only the casting light transmits**, area panels don't, and every blur gate (strength 0 / no caster / field carriers) leaves the term byte-identical. The transmitted rim on a deep body is a tight bright feature, so an overdriven radius prints the blur's tap comb around it exactly as around a tight highlight (honest radii can't show it — measured, ARCHITECTURE.md). Envelope: solid/textured meshes on the main canvas (a target's mesh and a raymarched field note once and keep plain shading; specular blurs with the diffuse, the single-buffer form). Example `3D/Materials/Subsurface` (space = bare comparison; castShadows on, so the swinging key transmits when behind); snapshots `subsurface-scattering` + `subsurface-transmittance`; `ScatterKernelTests` + `ScatteringRenderProbes` (terminator carry / red-outruns-blue / radius scaling / occlusion byte-identity / off-surface bit-exactness / reproducibility — verified red by sabotage) + `TransmittanceRenderProbes` (thin-vs-thick per caster kind / red-through-body / no-caster + strength-0 gates / reproducibility — the spot probe isolates the linearization, all verified red by sabotage). Mechanism: ARCHITECTURE.md § *Subsurface scattering*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#subsurface-scattering`. **Image-based lighting (IBL)** — `environment(_:)` / `Environment` lights the PBR materials from a surrounding HDRI via the **split-sum** bake (Karis; equirect→cube / irradiance / GGX-prefiltered mips / BRDF LUT, baked once per source and cached): **8 CC0 HDRIs bundled at 1K** (instant, offline, CI-safe) + **12 download-on-demand** (Poly Haven), user `.hdri(path:)` / `.hdri(downloadURL:)` / `.remote(url:fallbackResource:)`, `highRes(.twoK/.fourK/.eightK)` backdrop upgrades (the lighting never needs >1K), and the **procedural `.sky(turbidity:sunElevation:groundAlbedo:)`** — a zero-asset Hosek-Wilkie dome (vendored `External/CHosekWilkie`, BSD-3) generated with **no CPU read-back** (the read-back was the 3-fps bug), re-baked per frame at a reduced budget when animated (the HDRI path stays byte-identical), `rotated(_:)` spinning the sun for free; a neutral `.sky()` is the default placeholder while a download lands. **A live feed lights the scene** — `Environment.feed(_ feed: VideoFeed)` (the `.feed(id:)` source + the weak main-actor `FeedEnvironments` registry, `EnvironmentFeed.swift`): the feed's latest frame wraps into a GPU equirect (`ollin_ibl_feed_gen` — front hemisphere direct about +Z, back its horizontal mirror, pinched to the picture's center column at the poles, so the wrap is seamless) and runs the reduced-budget sky bake on the frame's command buffer (no CPU read-back). **Load-bearing:** the bake key carries a per-feed generation (`IBLKey.feedGeneration`, 0 for every other source) bumped on a new frame — newness is Image *identity*, and `FeedBakeState` **holds** the compared frame so a freed frame's recycled address can never read as "unchanged"; a GPU-backed frame (a live texture updated in place) re-bakes every resolve, the animated sky's own cost, and stale generations evict (the animated-sky discipline); `bakeReady`'s HDRI/sky path is textually untouched (the feed branches out first; the whole suite passed unrecorded); auto-exposure is **exactly 1** (a camera exposes itself; a real average would read every frame back); the factory sets `showsBackground` **false** (the wrap is for lighting, not viewing — draw the frame yourself); no frame yet / a deallocated feed → the neutral placeholder sky (the remote-download discipline). Example `3D/Environments/LiveEnvironment`; `FeedEnvironmentTests` (5, the color probe sabotage-verified); no pixel snapshot (the input is live). `Docs/3D/3D.md#live-environment`. **Volumetric clouds ride the sky bake** (`Environment.clouds(_:)`, the `Clouds` value: coverage/density/scale/tallness/phase, presets `.fair`/`.scattered`/`.broken`/`.overcast`) — the Schneider/Nubis recipe marched inside the sky generation so backdrop + IBL + reflections see one weather and an overcast dims the scene by construction; **load-bearing:** `ollin_ibl_sky_gen` stays verbatim with the clouds twin beside it (the GI codegen rule), the bake cache keys on **`IBLKey`** (source + clouds; clouds nil for non-sky sources so a stray value can't fragment an HDRI's slot — the cache-key sabotage is red without it), the weather field **thresholds fbm into hard-cored blobs** (a soft field reads as veil everywhere), the tiling noise volumes are a **fixed lattice baked once per process** (`ollin_cloud_noise_*`, deterministic on any machine), the march jitter keys on the fragment's **own pixel position** (a hardcoded texel scale printed stripe combs when the cloudy bake doubled to 2048), the skybox auto soft-focus turns off **only when the source is really `.sky` with clouds** (keying on the clouds value alone changed an HDRI's backdrop, a probe-caught live defect), and `phase` is the deterministic wind clock (a still sky bakes once and costs nothing per frame). Example `3D/Environments/Cloudscape`; snapshot `sky-clouds`; `CloudTests`. Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *The procedural sky*. Terse invariants (mechanism, caches, and downloads in [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *3D lighting and environments*): **mip the equirect *before* the equirect→cube pass** (else the cube reads empty → black IBL, a real bug); the bundled set is **PIZ half-float** (ImageIO won't decode dwab); **per-environment auto-exposure** (the set ranges ~800× in brightness) so any HDRI just works; non-PBR materials take the diffuse irradiance as their ambient (Gooch keeps its own ramp) so one environment lights every material — all gated on `iblEnabled` → **byte-identical with no environment**; export **blocks** on downloads (full-res art) while live shows a placeholder; the decoded equirect is blob-cached (`<hash>.equirectf16`) and read **off the render thread**; the in-memory bake cache is LRU-bounded (`IBLCacheTests`). The environment draws as a **skybox** backdrop by default (bicubic-reconstructed, resolution-aware `backgroundBlur`; `.lightingOnly()` opts out). (`Environment` is the bare public name, so Ollin's SwiftUI files qualify `@SwiftUI.Environment`, as they already do for `Image`.) **`matcap(_:)` / `Matcap`** (view-normal sphere-texture shading, 26 bundled CC0 looks + a CPU generator). **Meshes from file** — `loadMesh` / `Mesh(contentsOf:)`: Ollin's own OBJ + glTF readers beside Model I/O (`.usdz` / `.usdc` / `.usda` / `.stl` / `.ply` / `.abc`), carrying base-color material + texture + UVs; `bounds` / `center` / `normalized(scale:)` to recenter+fit. **Textured meshes** (`Mesh.uvs` / `MeshMaterial` / `mesh.textured(_:)`), **wireframe** (`wireframe(_:)`). **Normal maps** — `Mesh.normalMapped(_:scale:)` / `MeshMaterial.normalTexture`+`normalScale` / `Mesh.tangents` (`MeshTangent`, aligned with `positions`; generated by **vendored MikkTSpace**, `External/CMikkTSpace`, zlib — the glTF-named standard, bundled because matching its exact basis is the point). **Load-bearing:** the normal-mapped mesh is the textured path's **twin pipeline** (`ollin_mesh_nm_vertex`/`_fragment`, a `normalMapped` PipelineKey variant — never a branch in the shipped textured fragment, the verbatim-plus-twin codegen rule, so unmapped frames are byte-identical *by construction*); the tangent packs as **half4 in `OllinMeshVertex`'s 8-byte tail pad** (`OllinHalf4`: Metal `half4` / CPU `simd_ushort4` bit patterns — stride stays 64), world-space via the model's **linear part** (a surface direction, not the normal's inverse-transpose; skins pose it with the blended joint matrix); **stored handedness is glTF's sign = −fSign** — measured, raw MikkTSpace makes `fSign·cross(N,T)` point along +∂p/∂v (image-*down*) on every surface while the spec's maps are green-up (image-up), so the ecosystem stores the negation — verified against the Khronos NormalTangentTest/MirrorTest oracles (authored `TANGENT`s pass through unchanged); a **winding-vs-normals-inconsistent mesh flips fSign** (never build a convention probe on one), and a "missing terminator" between spheres at ±x is perspective parallax, not a bug (both cost real debugging); the map samples as **data, never color** (`Image.linearTexture(for:)`, the raw-bytes twin cache slot — an sRGB decode bends every stored direction); `finish.normalScale` (a claimed `OllinMaterial` tail pad) is set per batch only after uvs *and* tangents verify aligned, doubling as the encode and shader gate, with **0 routed down the plain textured pipeline byte-identically** (probe-pinned) and a missing basis degrading honestly (geometric normals + noteOnce); shading uses the bent normal everywhere, the **geometric** normal keeps driving ray/shadow/transmittance offsets; glTF reads `normalTexture` (+`scale`) and authored `TANGENT` on both merge paths, generating per spec when absent — but a generation that would *split* vertices (a mirrored-UV seam) is refused on skinned/morph/part-aligned meshes (their arrays ride the vertex order); mesh rebuilds (`welded`, `subdivided`) drop tangents and `generatingTangents()` re-attaches.
