Example `3D/Materials/NormalMaps` (maps authored from height functions); snapshot `normal-maps`; `MeshTangentTests` (12: basis pins + the green-direction/transform-bake/scale-0 render probes). Mechanism + the sign-convention record: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Normal mapping*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#normal-maps`. **Surface maps (the PBR map set)** — `Mesh.surfaceMapped(metallicRoughness:occlusion:occlusionStrength:emissive:emissiveColor:)` / the `MeshMaterial` map fields: a packed metallic-roughness map (glTF layout: roughness g, metallic b), an occlusion map (r), and an emissive map × `emissiveFactor`, all through one pipeline (`ollin_mesh_maps_fragment` over the shared nm vertex, the normal-map bend folded in behind its own gate). **Load-bearing:** per-pixel metallic/roughness reach the shading tail through **`meshLitColorMapped` / `ollin_pbr_ibl_ambient_mapped`, mechanical substitution copies of the shipped tails KEPT IN SYNC BY HAND** (the biggest such twin; edit the original, re-copy, re-substitute `mat.metallic`→`pxMetal` / `clamp((float)mat.roughness`→`clamp(pxRough` + the `pxAO` ambient scales — never grow the shipped originals, whose codegen every unmapped frame depends on); the sampled channels **multiply** (finish × the mesh material's factors × map — the drawer folds the mesh factors in only under `mrGate`, so `material(.physicallyBased(metallic: 1, roughness: 1))`, the new factory, shows a file's maps as authored); **occlusion dims indirect light only** (flat ambient in the twin, IBL/GI/flat-IBL at the fragment; direct untouched, pinned by a byte-equality counterfactual); **emissive adds after lighting, before the atmosphere** (a factor with no map routes down the same pipeline against a white stand-in; a black factor with a map emits nothing, the glTF default); mr/occlusion sample as **data** (`linearTexture`), emissive as **color**; gates ride the finish (`mrGate`/`occlusionStrength`/an `emissive` float4, claiming the last `OllinMaterial` pads + one appended row) so unmapped frames keep their exact codegen by construction. glTF reads the maps + factors/strength; **USD reads connected `metallic`/`roughness`/`occlusion`/`emissiveColor`/`normal` inputs** (an ORM-layout tap reuses the image — `USDAssetStore` caches decodes by path so taps compare `===` — else channels **repack** at load, untapped = white; factors ride tap `scale`, occlusion strength scale s / bias 1−s, normal strength scale (2s,2s,2,1) / bias (−s,−s,−1,0), each exact; USD normal maps generate tangents post-skinning under glTF's vertex-split guard); the spatial exporter writes every encoding back (reader's stated inverse; `usdchecker --arkit` accepts a mapped package, test-pinned). Envelope: primary surface only (reflection hits keep the per-vertex w-slot values; matcap/wireframe ignore the set). Example `3D/Materials/SurfaceMaps`; snapshot `surface-maps`; `SurfaceMapTests` (13: loaders/round-trip/validator + the metallic-split, roughness-spread, indirect-only-AO, unlit-emissive, factor-multiply and gate byte-identity probes — the shader sabotages each verified red). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *The PBR map set*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#surface-maps`. **Height maps (parallax occlusion + CPU displacement)** — `MeshMaterial.heightTexture`/`heightScale` + `Mesh.parallaxMapped(_:scale:)` / `Mesh.displaced(by:scale:)`: one height map (r channel, data; **white = the authored surface, darker carves in** — the shared datum, so the shading fake and the real geometry agree) read as per-pixel parallax (linear search + one secant step in the maps fragment — slice-2 code exempt from the verbatim rule, gated on the packed `parallax` finish field so mapless frames keep their codegen; the shifted uv feeds *every* map sample; height at texture 21, `usesSurfaceMaps` routes it) or as real geometry (**weld-aware**: one averaged normal + height per welded position group, so the flat-shaded generators' coincident corners move together and a uv seam can't tear; smooth normals recomputed over the welded displaced surface, tangents regenerated). **Load-bearing:** the uv step per unit depth is `scale/e.z · (−e.x, +e.y)` — the v sign is *opposite* u's because the stored-handedness bitangent points image-up while v grows down (the slice-1 green-up fact; probe-pinned per axis, sign-flip sabotage fails each independently); march samples use explicit `level(0)` (the loop's exit is divergent, implicit derivatives undefined); the `1/e.z` stretch floors at 0.1 (grazing views smear boundedly — the documented silhouette envelope); **parallax is shading only** — depth, silhouette, shadow rays, and reflections keep the flat surface (coverage-mask-equality-pinned, the teaching contrast with `displaced`); a white map is the exact null (byte-pinned on the same pipeline) and `heightScale` 0 routes down the plain textured path byte-identically; needs uvs + tangents like the normal map (`parallaxMapped` generates them; honest degrade with a note). glTF has no height-map slot (core or ratified extensions) so the map is Ollin-authored API there; **USD round-trips it** via the preview surface's `displacement` input (writer: a tap at scale s / bias −s, the exact spelling of s·(h−1); reader recovers `heightScale` from the channel scale, generates tangents for a displacement connection, and ignores a constant unconnected displacement — no relief in it; `usdchecker --arkit`-validated). Example `3D/Materials/Parallax` (one crater map: parallax / displaced / bare, depth as a live knob); snapshot `parallax-relief`; `ParallaxTests` (12: direction per axis, silhouette hold, both nulls, weld/datum/seam, USD round trip — sabotage-verified red). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Height maps: parallax occlusion and displacement*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#height-maps`; Guide Ch 21 § *Depth from a picture: height maps* + HeightRelief figure. **Triplanar projection (texture with no uvs)** — `Mesh.triplanarTextured(_:normal:scale:normalScale:baseColor:)` / `MeshMaterial.triplanarScale` (world units per tile; 0 = uv mapping): the base texture (and a normal map) project flat along the three world axes and blend by the surface normal, for the meshes that carry no uvs at all (isosurface/metaballs, growth, subdivision, reconstruction) — no uvs, no tangents, none generated. Rides the surface-mapped fragment behind a `mat.triplanar` gate (tiles per world unit in the tail pad parallax left, stride unchanged; the slice-2 branch-freely exemption re-verified empirically: full snapshot suite unrecorded + guide-figures zero churn). **Load-bearing:** the sampling space is **world space, decided** (the drawer bakes the model matrix CPU-side; the envelope stated everywhere: abutting meshes continue one pattern, a transform-animated mesh slides through it, pinned by `theProjectionIsAnchoredToTheWorldNotTheMesh`; a `space:` param can widen later); each axis's **u flips with the normal's side** so the picture reads unmirrored from either direction (keyed on the *normal*, not the viewer — the back of a single-sided quad legitimately mirrors, a probe-caught lesson), v runs down the image, each frame right-handed so the normal map keeps its green-up decode; weights are |n|⁴ renormalized; the per-plane normal combine adds the tangent-plane push onto the geometric normal expressed in the plane's frame and multiplies the heights (a flat map returns exactly the geometric normal); samples through its own repeat constexpr sampler (the mesh sampler clamps). **v1 carries base color + normal only** — MR/occlusion/emissive/height stay uv-mapped (noteOnce), parallax is the technique-level cut (one uv-space march can't ride three projections); constant emissive + `material(_:)` finish compose. Neither glTF nor USD has a triplanar slot → Ollin-authored API only; the spatial recorder notes and strips the projected maps. Example `3D/Materials/Triplanar`; snapshot `triplanar`; `TriplanarTests` (9, the u-flip / hard-pick / top-v / green-direction sabotages verified red). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Triplanar projection*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#triplanar`; Guide Ch 21 § *A picture from three sides: triplanar* + TriplanarSkin figure. **Detail maps** — `Mesh.detailMapped(_:normal:scale:strength:)` / `MeshMaterial.detailTexture`+`detailNormalTexture`+`detailScale`+`detailStrength`: a finer texture pair tiled `detailScale` times across the base uv in the surface-mapped fragment (the branch-freely exemption again; textures 22/23, both **data** reads through a repeat sampler). **Load-bearing:** the color map's neutral is **byte 128 in a data read** (the sample ×2 multiplies; an sRGB read would put the neutral at ~188, which no author would guess) so a flat-128 map is near-identity and **`strength: 0` is the exact off switch** (the drawer never raises the gates → plain-pipeline byte-identity, probe-pinned); the detail normal blends by **Reoriented Normal Mapping** (a flat detail hands back exactly the base normal — the identity an overwrite blend fails, probe-pinned) and its branch **recomputes the bent normal from scratch** (one redundant base sample only when detail is on) so the shipped base-normal resolve stays textually untouched; `OllinMaterial` grew 256→272 (`detailScale`/`detailStrength` claimed the tail pads + a `detailGates` row) and the `usesSurfaceMaps` union gate is `detailScale > 0`, packed only for a verified uv-mapped mesh (never with triplanar; the detail-normal half needs tangents like a normal map, each half degrading honestly alone); no mips like the whole mesh texture path, so extreme tile counts shimmer (documented envelope); no glTF/USD slot → Ollin-authored only, the exporter notes-and-strips. Example `3D/Materials/Detail`; snapshot `detail-maps`; `DetailMapTests` (sabotage-verified). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Detail maps*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#detail-maps`. **Decals** — `Decal` (the `LightCookie` construction + kept `aspect`) + the per-frame `decal(_:at:direction:width:height:depth:roll:opacity:)`: a picture stamped through an oriented box onto every lit mesh surface inside it, composited over **albedo before lighting** (the stamp takes the surface's own finish), capped at `OLLIN_MAX_DECALS` (8), repeated placements sharing a texture layer, later decals compositing over earlier in call order. **Load-bearing:** the decal loop lives **only in the surface-mapped fragment**, and a frame with placed decals **routes every lit mesh batch through that pipeline at encode time** (wireframe/matcap/grid keep their own) — no shipped fragment grows, so a no-decal frame is byte-identical **by construction** (a decal-carrying frame shading plain meshes through the maps fragment is the feature's own envelope); solid meshes ride the nm vertex safely because `OllinMeshVertex()` zero-fills; the CPU builds each world→unit-box transform once (`Drawer.placeDecal`, the cookie projector-frame convention, +y in box space = the image's top); the composite is **premultiplied with one scale on both halves** (fades can't fringe the edge), a **facing fade** melts the stamp off edge-on surfaces instead of smearing it, and a depth-end fade keeps the box's far planes from hard-clipping; the list rides its own `OllinDecals` uniform (fragment buffer 2, count-gated) beside a content-hash-cached sRGB `texture2d_array` at texture 24 (`shapingStandIn()` when empty); reflections show the undecaled base, fields/particles/point clouds sit outside by design, and the spatial exporter notes placed decals stayed behind. Example `3D/Materials/Decals` (declares `loopDuration`); snapshot `decals`; `DecalTests` (placement pinned against a drawn marker at the same world position; sabotage-verified). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Projected decals*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#decals`. **Cast shadows** — `castShadows()` / `noShadows()`: directional/spot via a 2D shadow map; point **ray-traced** on an RT GPU (inline `intersection_query` behind `OLLIN_RT_SHADOWS`), else a mid-point cube; rect/disk area panels ray-traced on an RT GPU, else a spot-style 2D map (the *Area cast shadows* rules above); `shadowQuality(_:)` maps hardware-relative ray counts. **Soft (PCSS) shadows** — `shadowSoftness(_:)` (0 hard → the legacy 3×3, byte-identical; 0.5 default contact-hardening; 1 very soft): blocker search → penumbra estimate → Vogel-disk PCF (`shadowFactorPCSS`, the Fernando/NVIDIA technique); **the penumbra ratio must be formed in light-linear depth** (a spot map linearizes via the projection's `[2][2]` term — details in ARCHITECTURE.md); the one knob also drives the RT point caster's area radius (the 0.5 default reproduces the old look, `point-shadows` byte-identical); 2D taps via `resolveShadowTaps2D` (export → `.detail`); soft-by-default measured sub-tolerance, so no snapshot re-records (the SSAO silhouette-AA precedent). **Contact shadows** — `contactShadows(length:)` / `noContactShadows()`, per-frame state refining whichever caster `castShadows()` resolved (every caster kind, any Metal GPU): a depth pre-pass re-renders the solid canvas meshes from the camera (the `.meshShadow` pipeline fed the camera's jittered VP, the scatter-mask filter), then a fullscreen march (`ollin_contact_shadow`, ShaderCombine) walks each pixel's projected (uv, z) segment toward the caster and the mesh carriers sample the mask (fragment texture 16) into the same `meshFieldShadow` multiplier the marched fields ride, so `meshLitColor` is untouched. **Load-bearing, each a probed defect or a production-reference rule:** the ray start is pulled **0.002 of the eye distance toward the camera** (the no-normals self-occlusion guard; without it a static speckled crescent forms along every lit silhouette) and the occlusion test is a **band** around a threshold derived from a same-length view-axis ray's own device-z span scaled by `max(0.07, 1/steps)` (slope- and density-aware, so a thin edge can't streak); a first-hit binary verdict + **static** interleaved-gradient-noise start dither (the dither rule; the production reference's own TAA-off configuration) + a clip-space vignette fade; `length` is world units, nil = 2.5% of the eye-to-target distance (packed in `makeLighting`'s framing block; the renderer zeroes the gate when the pass didn't run, so off / no-caster / targets / accumulation are byte-identical, suite-verified); steps = 8/16/32 by `effectiveQuality(.default)` (the TAA no-per-feature-knob rule; measured M2 release 1080²: the whole term ~1.0 ms GPU); the envelope is the screen (off-screen geometry casts nothing, a curved surface picks up a little extra shading inside its silhouette, only the casting light dims; fields and target-drawn meshes sit outside the march by design). Example `3D/Effects/ContactShadows`; snapshot `contact-shadows`; `ContactShadowRenderProbes` (sabotage-verified) + the `LightingTests` packing trio. `Docs/3D/3D.md#contact-shadows`; Guide Ch 21 § *Shadows, and what they tell you* + Seated figure. **Ray-traced reflections** (`rayTracedReflections()`) — a physically-based metal reflects the **actual scene** (off-screen geometry included, no SSR contact-streaks), the hybrid-rendering approach written from the technique (Apple's WWDC21 session + Metal sample). It **integrates into the IBL specular, not a post-process** (the traced hit replaces the prefiltered env sample; a miss shows the sky), with **two-bounce, metalness-aware** hit shading — the second trace is load-bearing for mirror corners: an unoccluded env sample at the first hit pipes the HDRI's bright floor through as a razor-thin streak no AA can remove (a real fixed bug; dead ends recorded in ARCHITECTURE.md) — and grazing-widened env lobes (`ollin_rt_env_lobe`), into which a **rough hit fades its own traced bounce** (`smoothstep(0.12, 0.55, s1.rough)`, the primary surface's rule, so a matte floor reads matte in a mirror and through glass instead of turning into a second mirror; the miss branch already *is* that lobe, and a hit below the ramp is untouched, so the near-mirror snapshots didn't move). It **reuses the shadow-caster accel** (per-geometry offsets buffer; `encodeShadowPass` decoupled so the accel builds when shadows *or* reflections are active), gated by `OllinLighting.rtReflections` → **byte-identical when off**; every solid mesh is reflectable; needs an environment + an RT GPU, off-by-default, non-RT → no-op. **Reflection AA is deferred + stochastic:** live temporally accumulates (an `SSRHistorySlot` twin, `statefulEncodeIsRepeat`-guarded); headless/export instead averages N deterministic in-frame rays (4/8/16 by tier — historyless, so no warmup and video exports can't flicker; snapshot `rt-reflections-3d` re-recorded once, supersampled). **Load-bearing:** render targets + the raymarched fields keep the inline single-ray trace (a thin wrapper over the shared hit shade, so the paths can't drift), and **no roughness-cone jitter without a spatial denoise stage** (a stochastic cone reads as sparkle on brushed metals). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *3D lighting and environments* + § *Deferred ray-traced reflection AA*. **Global illumination** (`globalIllumination(_:intensity:)` / `noGlobalIllumination()` / `globalIlluminationQuality(_:)`) — real-time diffuse bounce via a re-traced probe field (the DDGI papers, written independently; `ShaderGI.metal` + `encodeGIPass`): a probe grid auto-fitted to the mesh geometry's AABB (**per-axis counts adapt to the fitted volume's aspect** under a fixed 512 budget, axes clamped 2…16, derived at fit time by `giAxisCounts`' smallest-isotropic-spacing search and held with the volume — a cube keeps 8×8×8, a flat scene spends 16×2×16; atlases allocate once at budget capacity so a refit never reallocates; the bounds fold over the same batch walk as the accel, **target-drawn meshes included**, so a scene drawn entirely inside a render target still gets a field), each probe an 8×8 octahedral irradiance tile + 16×16 distance-moments tile (1-texel gutters recomputed in-pass by octahedral wrap), traced per update with a rotated spherical-Fibonacci fan against the shared accel and sampled by every lit carrier (textures 13/14/15) through trilinear × soft-backface × Chebyshev weights from a self-shadow-biased point. **Load-bearing:** under an environment the probe sample **replaces** the IBL diffuse irradiance (probes integrate the same sky, occlusion included) and specular is untouched; probes store **E/π in perceptual gamma 5** (sample = pow 2.5 per probe, square after the blend, the irradiance-cube-compatible convention); hit shading traces **one visibility ray to the casting light** (else bounce leaks through the wall whose shadow the direct pass draws) and adds the *previous* update's field (multi-bounce by recursion, packed at intensity 1 so the artistic dial can't compound); **never `mix` toward an unread history** (0·NaN poisoned the whole field, a real first-render bug), **visibility moments cap at 1.5× spacing** (uncapped far geometry collapses near-surface variance into hard dark blobs), and **probe relocation is not optional** (wall-slab rooms park whole probe rows inside the slabs; the relocate pass walks them out via backface statistics, the sign of a surfel's stored depth); **relocation statistics read the fixed, never-rotated fan** (the surfel texture's first 32 columns, distance-only; the blends skip them — fed the rotated radiance fan instead, its thresholds flicker and a probe near one oscillates between positions, visibly pulsing every surface its cage touches, a real live defect isolated by freezing relocation) and a comfortable probe **drifts back toward its grid anchor** with proposals committing only inside the 0.45-of-spacing ellipsoid; **the live irradiance blend's temporal-response pair is asymmetric and live-only** (a darkening past 0.25 cuts hysteresis by 0.75, a brightening past 0.10 luminance is rate-limited to a quarter step — with a sun disc in the fan a bright spike is estimator variance to absorb, not news to trust, and un-limited it pulses a sky-lit scene's whole field; headless skips the pair so its iterations converge an unbiased progressive mean — both live stabilizers are verified by live frame-diff measurement, not tests, the artificial-life honesty rule); live = one hysteresis-0.97 update/frame into ping-ponged atlases (`statefulEncodeIsRepeat`-guarded, volume **held** until bounds escape it; `beginStatefulEncode` runs the frame-scoped reset ahead of the GI pass, which precedes the effect-target encode), headless/export = per-frame refit + 8/12/16 whole iterations at progressive-mean hysteresis with iteration-indexed seeds → **byte-deterministic**; rays/probe and the headless iteration count resolve through the persistent **`globalIlluminationQuality(_:)` tier** (the `shadowQuality` shape; the probe *grid* deliberately never rides the tier — a tier-driven grid would move the probes and light an export differently from live); **traced mirror interiors and render targets gather too**: the shared hit shade (`ollin_rt_hit_radiance`) swaps its irradiance-cube diffuse for a field sample at the hit, **pre-divided by the IBL exposure** (the whole traced radiance re-scales by it on composite), atlases bound into the deferred trace pass and threaded through `ollin_pbr_ibl_ambient` inline, and targets take the field via `encodeEffectTargets(gi:)` — distinct from the flat-pastel-blobs dead end, which was about the *specular* secondary; RT devices only (no-op otherwise), byte-identical when off (suite-verified; `gi-3d` re-recorded once for the adaptive grid); remaining envelope: point clouds / particles don't gather. **Camera-anchored probe cascades** give a vast scene a near field: past the coarseness threshold (fitted spacing > half the eye-to-target distance — calibrated so a room with the camera *inside* stays single-volume, and it must) `giCascadeLadder` adds up to 3 windows halving the scene spacing toward a quarter of the working distance, stacked one 512-probe atlas slot each (same widths, so `ollin_gi_atlas_uv` and the carriers' bindings are untouched); axes whose span covers the scene slab **pin** (a terrain's cascades never scroll vertically); live they scroll in whole probe planes (phys = (grid + phase) mod counts, one-plane truncation dead zone) with a scroll pass invalidating exactly the scrolled-in planes via the offsets texture's **w validity**, which the blends read per probe (fresh at full weight, the refit rule per probe); headless refits ladder + volumes per frame (pure function of the frame, no scroll machinery); sampling takes the finest covering cascade, fading over its outermost cell into the next coarser, scene volume the fallback. **Codegen rule: `ollin_gi_sample` and `ollin_gi_trace` stay verbatim as the single-volume paths with cascaded twins beside them** (fast-math re-contracts unchanged expressions when a function's control flow grows, and the ulp is a byte off every dithered frame) — the no-cascade path is byte-identical (pixel-exact reference scenes + a 180-file snapshot re-record matching a clean-HEAD re-record byte-for-byte). Example `3D/Lighting/GlobalIllumination` (intensity + the quality tier as live knobs — `RenderQuality` conforms to `ParamOption`, so any quality dial can be a segmented `@Param`); snapshot `gi-3d` (RT-gated); `GlobalIlluminationTests` (11: counterfactual probes incl. mirror-interior + render-target + the knob's export-tier bytes + the exact count derivation, sabotage-verified red). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Global illumination*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#global-illumination`. **Depth-aware compositing** — `depth(at:)` / `noDepth()` / `project(_:)` / `withBillboard(at:){}` put 2D drawing inside the 3D depth buffer, or composite against a depth *feed* via `drawDepthScene(color:depth:…)` / the metric `drawDepthScene(_ frame: RGBDFrame)` + `Camera3D.fromIntrinsics` (a drawn cloud, a depth feed, and a placed object share one metric space). Wired depth-correct through live + headless `image(of:)`; accumulation / texture-handoff render 3D without depth sorting (documented limitation), and gradient paint isn't supported on the 3D path (solid color only). Building 3D drove the `Pipeline` enum → `Hashable PipelineKey` migration. `Docs/3D/3D.md`, `Docs/3D/DepthCompositing.md`; examples the `3D/*` set (PointCloud / DepthCloud / Transforms / Solids / Lighting / LoadedMesh / Shadows / RayTracedReflections / Materials / PhysicalMaterials / ImageBasedLighting / EnvironmentGallery / ProceduralSky / Matcap / DepthCompositing / MetricDepthScene / …); snapshots `sparkle-materials` / `pbr-materials` / `pbr-ibl` / `procedural-sky` / `rt-reflections-3d` (RT-gated via `Snapshot.hasRaytracing`, so it records/runs only on a ray-tracing GPU).

- **Caustics** (`caustics(intensity:dispersion:)` / `noCaustics()` / `causticsQuality(_:)`; `Renderer/ShaderCaustics.metal` + `MetalRenderer+Caustics.swift`). Photon-traced focused light: emit a budget of photons from the caster light, walk each through transmissive (`Material.glass`) and mirror-polished-metal surfaces with **photon differentials** (the emission cell's u/v perturbations chain-ruled through every reflect/refract, including the interpolated normal's own triangle-plane derivative, which is what makes curved glass focus), deposit on the first rough opaque hit, splat each as an **anisotropic elliptical footprint** into a screen-space layer the four lit mesh carriers add by screen position (fragment **texture 25**, `causticsEnabled`/`causticsScale` on the `OllinLighting` tail, the ltcEnabled discipline → **byte-identical off**, whole suite unrecorded). Adaptive emission per the published technique: a light-space **density map → per-texel perfect-square leaf counts → cumulative-uint4 quadtree** (node = running child sums in traversal order r=(0,0), g=+(1,0), b=+(0,1), w=total; the walk's final level read is guarded off) hands every GPU thread its emission cell; deposited photons atomically feed back **projected screen area and temporal variance** (the live temporal resolve writes variance into the layer's **alpha**, which next frame's photons sample — the loop closes through it), density updates as d·clamp(aAvg/aTarget) + v·gain with a 3×3 spatial blend and a probe floor. **Load-bearing:** photon flux is the **beam cross-section** — first-hit footprint area × |cos incidence| (area alone over-weights grazing silhouette rays into a skirt of bright spikes, a fixed real defect); a footprint past the **max screen extent is CULLED, never clamped** (the min-width clamp would widen a meters-thin streak into a visible hairline combing the floor — the second fixed defect; min-width itself stays area-conserving); deposit requires **≥ 1 specular bounce** (a straight light-to-surface path is the direct lighting the scene already shades — caustics never double it) and drops paths still inside a solid; the accel build **breaks its coalesced runs where the caustic materials change** and fills the parallel per-geometry `OllinCausticGeo` buffer (transmission/ior/thin/attenuation; metal/rough/albedo ride the baked vertex slots), only while caustics are on; glass = refract-only lineage (Fresnel-weighted, albedo tint at entry, Beer-Lambert over the traced interior span, TIR bounces inside), polished metal (metallic > 0.5, roughness < 0.25) = reflect, F0-tinted; **one caster light** by the shadow system's priority (shadowLight if punctual, else directional → spot → point; directional = ortho patch fitted to the specular casters' AABB, spot = its own cone, point = the cone subtending that AABB); splat shading uses the caustics G-buffer's own attributes (normal/metal-rough/albedo MRT + depth — the reflection G-buffer's recipe plus albedo, pipeline `.causticsGBuffer`) with Lambert + GGX toward the eye in the house units, and a **manual slope-adaptive depth compare** clips footprints to the geometry they lit; **export/headless = uniform emission at the detail budget, no history touched** (a frame is a pure function of itself; live = jittered emission + EMA resolve on an `SSRHistorySlot` twin behind `statefulEncodeIsRepeat`); dispersion = per-photon IOR perturbation (±3% × the knob) with energy-preserving RGB weights that sum to white. The kernels live in the **main** shader library (`libraryComputePipeline(_:)`, its cache cleared on live shader reload); segment order: after ShaderGI (needs Shader3D's `ollin_rt_query`/`ollin_rt_fetch_surface` and ShaderEffects' `PresentOut`), everything traced inside `#if OLLIN_RT_SHADOWS`; non-RT devices no-op (`causticsWanted` gates on `rayTracedShadows`). Documented v1 envelope: screen-space apply (no caustics seen in mirrors/through glass), roughness never widens differentials (sharp caustics from frosted glass), no area-light photons (the soft-caustics covariance extension is the follow-up), a glass surface's own specular spawns no second photon family. Example `3D/Lighting/Caustics` (lens spot inside its own shadow + chrome-torus fans, hold-space compare); snapshot `caustics` (RT-gated); `CausticRenderProbes` (inert-without-casters byte-equality, lens-brightens-its-shadow on/off diff, green-glass tint). `Docs/3D/Caustics.md`.
- **Temporal anti-aliasing** (`temporalAntialiasing()` / `noTemporalAntialiasing()`; `ollin_fx_taa_resolve` in `Renderer/ShaderCombine.metal` + `applyTemporalAA` in `MetalRenderer+Targets.swift`). Sub-pixel projection jitter accumulated across frames (live) or averaged within the frame (export), refining 3D edges past MSAA and absorbing the screen-space/traced effects' residual shimmer. Per-frame state like `rayTracedReflections()`; **main canvas + active 3D camera only** (2D no-op with a note; `withTarget` layers and the accumulation surface keep plain MSAA); any Metal GPU. The jitter is **one NDC translate premultiplied onto the projection** (`MetalRenderer.jittered(_:by:)` — exact for perspective/ortho/intrinsic where the add-to-[2][0] trick is perspective-only; z/w untouched, so `depth(at:)` and every depth test are unchanged; zero jitter early-outs, so the off path is byte-identical **by construction** — whole snapshot suite + a zero-churn `guide-figures.sh` run verified it at the byte level), threaded as one `taaJitter` param through every main-canvas 3D pass (`encode`'s `makeUniforms3D`, the reflect G-buffer, the scatter mask, both half-res field pre-passes; targets pass zero). **Load-bearing, each a measured live defect first:** both reprojection matrices are **unjittered** (the published remove-the-jitter velocity rule — reconstructing through the jittered inverse resamples the history at a different sub-pixel offset every frame and its content random-walks with variance Var(jitter)/(1−feedback²): px-scale oscillation on every hairline edge; the RT-reflect temporal takes the same rule now that its G-buffer carries the frame's jitter); the rectification box is **velocity-adaptive** (γ 2.5 at rest easing to 0.75 by 15 px/frame — at fixed γ=1 the μ±σ box itself oscillates with the jitter phase and drags a converged hairline edge, the clamp-sawtooth failure); adaptive feedback keys on **how far the clip moved the history** (untouched → 0.97, dragged → 0.88), never on the instantaneous jittered sample, which permanently distrusts exactly the pixels needing the longest memory. Live accumulates into an `SSRHistorySlot` twin (`taaHistory`) behind `statefulEncodeIsRepeat`, fed by a `.min` main-depth resolve (attached only when TAA runs) and a Catmull-Rom history resample; headless/export renders **N deterministic jittered geometry passes averaged in linear light** (4/8/16 by tier; export's automatic tier is `.detail` = 16) with the pre-passes (shadows, GI, layers, sims, the deferred reflection) outside the loop — byte-stable snapshots, flicker-free video, frame-grab-safe. The live stabilizers carry no test (the artificial-life honesty rule; every deterministic probe restarts the loop): verification is the frame-diff record in ARCHITECTURE.md (TAA-off control exactly 0.0; converged hairline-rod crop p99 3–11/255, down from 14–33). `TAATests` pins the deterministic half — the **kernel-discrimination probe** (the export average must match its own analytic construction, built by box-reducing an 8× reference at each declared offset in **linear light**; a plain box-filtered ground truth is *not* the export's limit, its kernel is box ⊗ jitter set, and judging AA in gamma space is the classic trap), flat-interior energy conservation, on-then-off byte-equality, two-render determinism, the 2D gate, and the exact-pixel-shift unit — discrimination + conservation verified red by sabotage. **Per-object motion vectors** — `withMotion { }` / `withMotion("name") { }` declares the block's meshes one world-space mover: the drawer remembers each draw's model matrix across frames under **call site + occurrence + draw index** (the `SSRSlotKey` rule, never a frame-wide ordinal; history prunes when a key skips a frame, and a first sighting records nothing) and records `prevModel·inverse(curModel)` per vertex range; `encodeVelocityPass` writes an **rg16Float velocity texture — previous − current, pixels, y-down, both view·projections unjittered** (the MetalFX motion contract verbatim; one buffer designed for TAA + the temporal upscaler + motion blur, each now a shipped consumer) with a sentinel clear (`OLLIN_VELOCITY_NONE`; a NaN from a degenerate transform reads unwritten), in **two phases: non-movers depth-only first** (nil fragment + empty color mask — a hidden mover must lose the depth test; wireframes skip both phases) then the mover ranges; the resolve samples at its dilated closest-depth neighbor (closest-fetch) and a written texel replaces the matrix fallback, which stays **verbatim** — population is **sparse movers-only by design** (every no-mover pixel keeps the shipped live-measured path, byte-identical by construction); a stationary mover writes the camera's own motion (the total-motion contract, probe-pinned). Live-only for TAA (export supersamples within the frame and never reprojects, so under TAA alone `withMotion` costs an export nothing; the motion-blur chain re-encodes the same pass headless when it needs velocities). `VelocityBufferTests` (13: registry rules + velocity-texture readback probes over a 1:1 ortho scene + a crafted-texture resolve-branch probe; the velocity-sign / occluder-drop / resolve-sign sabotages each verified red); the live perceptual A/B is **measured** (static-camera probe, 30-capture bursts per mode: mover edge raggedness 0.006 with exact motion vs 0.048 without, 3–10× at every matched bar length, control rod identical, no ghost either way — record in ARCHITECTURE.md). Example `3D/Effects/TemporalAA` (the orbiting bar's `exactMotion` knob); snapshot `taa`. Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Temporal anti-aliasing*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#temporal-antialiasing`.

- **Motion blur** (`motionBlur(shutter:)` / `noMotionBlur()`; `ollin_mb_*` in `Renderer/ShaderCombine.metal` + `applyMotionBlur` in `MetalRenderer+Targets.swift`). The published velocity-buffer reconstruction filter (credited in `ATTRIBUTION.md`): a full-screen **velocity fill** (the `withMotion` mover texture composited over depth-reprojected camera motion per pixel — the temporal upscaler consumes the same field through its own unscaled variant, the blur's copy being half-shutter-scaled and magnitude-clamped to the published `[0.5px, k]`), the **TileMax → NeighborMax dominant-velocity pyramid** (k = clamp(height/36, 16, 64), the published 20 px at 720 tall made resolution-relative), and the **three-case reconstruction gather** (cone / cylinder / soft-depth-compare, S taps = 9/15/27 by `effectiveQuality(.default)` — the TAA no-per-feature-knob rule; the gather jitter is a pure function of pixel position, the dither's rule). Per-frame state like TAA; same envelope (main canvas + active 3D camera only, 2D no-op with a note, targets/accumulation/texture-handoff excluded); any Metal GPU; runs **after the TAA resolve, before the frame filters** (linear pre-tonemap, so bloom reads the streaks). **Load-bearing:** the cross-frame camera lives on the **Drawer** (`previousCamera3D`, kept in `beginFrame`), so live, same-frame repeats, and headless export all read the same previous matrix — the TAA slot's copy is the identical value on a live frame, and on a **repeat** the slot has already advanced, which is exactly why the blur re-encodes the mover pass itself through the shared `encodeMoverVelocity` core (the TAA `encodeVelocityPass` gates are **untouched**, so the shipped TAA path stays byte-identical); export works because the headless drive already runs `performDraw()` for every warmup frame, so the mover registry and previous camera populate with no extra machinery, and frame 0 (no previous camera) renders unblurred by definition; a **still frame skips on the CPU** (previous view-projection float-equal + no mover ranges → byte-identical), and a still *mover* passes through the GPU early-out byte-identically; the **backdrop (depth exactly 1) holds still by design** (2D drawing, the clear, the environment skybox never smear — the TAA background rule); soft-depth extent = 1% of the eye→target `sceneScale` proxy (a screen-space constant would hide a scale assumption, the SSS lesson); the depth resolve rides the TAA `.min` attachment, gate widened to `taaActive || blurActive` (export allocates its own). Shutter 0.5 = the 180-degree film standard; the fill stores **current − previous × half-shutter** (the paper's half-spread, sign-exact). Point clouds/particles take camera blur only (no per-particle velocity). Example `3D/Effects/MotionBlur`; snapshot `motion-blur` (frame 2 — a mover + camera pan, any Metal GPU); `MotionBlurTests` (12: streak-direction twins, tile-pyramid reach, shutter scaling, camera-only blur, the still/frame-0/2D byte-identity gates, determinism — the neighbor-max skip, velocity-axis swap, and mover-drop sabotages each verified red). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Motion blur*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#motion-blur`.

- **Temporal upscaling** (`temporalUpscaling(_ quality:)` / `noTemporalUpscaling()`; `MetalRenderer+Upscaling.swift` + `ollin_fx_velocity_fill` in `Renderer/ShaderCombine.metal`). The live window renders the whole frame at a reduced size and the platform temporal scaler (MetalFX) reconstructs the full canvas from the jittered history: the tier maps to a render fraction (`.performance` half per axis / `.default` two-thirds / `.detail` three-quarters, clamped to the device range), per-pixel cost falls by the square, edges stay temporally anti-aliased. Per-frame state like TAA; same envelope (main canvas + active 3D camera, 2D no-op with a note, targets/accumulation keep MSAA) plus a `supportsDevice` gate (Apple silicon; elsewhere note + full res). **Load-bearing:** every geometry-side pass sizes to the *render* size (the logical canvas never changes and the encode sets no explicit viewport, so shrinking the attachments is the whole change; off, the sizes are equal and the frame is byte-identical by construction); the scaler **replaces** `applyTemporalAA` (it *is* the jittered accumulation — feeding it the averaged output would integrate twice) and drops `taaHistory` while it runs; its motion texture is the **unscaled** velocity fill (raw previous − current pixels y-down, both VPs unjittered — the buffer's designed contract at `motionVectorScale` 1; the raw-not-scaled passthrough is test-pinned), its jitter offsets are the content's own pixel shifts from the shared Halton table **extended 16 → 32** (cycling `min(32, ceil(8·s²))` phases; TAA's first-8 cycle and the export's ≤16 prefix are untouched, prefix test-pinned); motion blur under it runs at output size reading the render-res depth/mover by UV with the mover values rescaled by a `moverScale` whose (1,1) default multiplies exactly; a **same-frame repeat returns the slot's output without re-encoding** (the scaler's internal history must advance once per frame, the `taaHistory` rule); creation failure flips the support flag (note once, never retry per frame). **Headless/export/frame-grab never upscale**: `headlessTemporalAAActive` routes an upscaling sketch to the full-res deterministic TAA supersample, pinned **byte-identical** to asking for `temporalAntialiasing()` (the export is the full-quality equivalent of the live preview; the GPU benchmark also stays full-res). Live verification is measurement (the artificial-life honesty rule): converged static-scene frame diffs 0.08-0.11/255 mean (inside the TAA-on band, and stiller than un-upscaled because the scaler absorbs the RT-reflection sparkle), FPS A/B 29 → 60 (vsync-capped) at `.performance` on the M2 probe scene; record in ARCHITECTURE.md. Example `3D/Effects/Upscaling` (tier + toggle knobs); `UpscalingTests` (9, fill + gate sabotages verified red; no pixel snapshot — the scaler is a stateful platform object). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Temporal upscaling*. `Docs/3D/3D.md#temporal-upscaling`.

- **Scene import** (`Sources/Ollin/3D/Scene.swift` + the `GLTFDocument` split in `MeshLoaderGLTF.swift`). `loadScene` / `Scene(contentsOf:)` is the structure-preserving complement to `loadMesh`: a glTF/GLB file's default scene as a tree of **`SceneNode`** values (name, authored local transform kept **verbatim as a matrix** — never decomposed, so nothing is lost to a TRS round-trip; an optional `Mesh` in node-local space, that node's primitives merged by the `loadMesh` rules) plus the authored **cameras** (core glTF) and **punctual lights** (`KHR_lights_punctual`) resolved through their nodes' world transforms into ordinary `Camera3D` / `Light` values the sketch applies itself (`camera(scene.camera ?? …)`, `light(_:)` each); the **USD family** (`.usdz`/`.usdc`/`.usda`/`.usd`, `SceneLoaderUSD.swift`) is read **natively end to end** — one `USDStage` parse serves structure, meshes, cameras, materials, lights, animation, and skinning; Model I/O is out of the USD path entirely. **Load-bearing:** the node tree keeps **authored child order** and every node carries **per-prim identity** (`sourceIndex`, assigned depth-first in authored order — what every track binds by, so a name duplicated across branches animates exactly the prim that authored the samples); meshes rebuild from the authored data — **indexed on the authored points** normally, **expanded per face corner** when normals or `primvars:st` are faceVarying/per-face (the deforming form must stay indexed, so there such a set drops; `primvars:normals` wins over `normals`, `primvars:st:indices` dereferences and a blocked (`None`) indices attr reads as unauthored, fan triangulation in authored winding, `leftHanded` reverses, a bad-index face skips whole); **materials read the bound `UsdPreviewSurface` spec-correctly** — `inputs:diffuseColor` re-encodes **linear→sRGB** (the glTF-factor treatment; the fixture scripts author linear literals computed from the designed display colors, so the committed assets kept their look and no snapshot re-recorded) and a **connected `UsdUVTexture` resolves its image through the usdz package entries** or the layer's folder (`USDAssetStore`), `primvars:displayColor` the fallback; cameras resolve from the prims' authored attributes (fov = 2·atan(verticalAperture / 2·focalLength), ortho height = verticalAperture/10, near/far from `clippingRange`) through the **shared** `resolveCamera(projection:near:far:world:sceneCenter:)` tail; **`visibility = "invisible"` hides its subtree and a `guide`/`proxy` purpose skips render** (nodes stay in the tree; meshes and lights skip — the lights ride the same walk, so a hidden light stays dark); **`upAxis`/`metersPerUnit` are deliberately not applied** (a scene arrives as-authored, the `loadMesh` contract; probed: the platform importer ignored both too); Material/Shader/NodeGraph/Skeleton/SkelAnimation/BlendShape/GeomSubset prims never become nodes (each is consumed by its own pass); a subdivision-surface mesh draws its **control cage** (`mesh.subdivided(_:)` is the user's refinement path); the raw tree's format-dependent shapes (`.token` vs `.string`, tuple arrays) normalize at this consumer tier through the shared `usd*` accessors on `USDValue` — new USD consumers use those, never re-switch. **`Mesh.loadUSD`** is the merged form over the same walk with node transforms **baked into the vertices** (the platform merge read local vertex data, so a multi-part file's parts piled at the origin — a real fixed defect); first authored material wins, UVs all-or-nothing; STL/PLY/ABC keep `loadViaModelIO`. **USD lights** (`SceneLoaderUSDLights.swift`: the walk attaches node-riding specs for the UsdLux prims — SphereLight→point, +`shaping:cone:angle`→spot (half-angle° ×2 → `coneAngle`, softness → `penumbra`), DistantLight→directional, RectLight/DiskLight/CylinderLight→rect/disk/tube area lights (extents scale with the prim's transform; cylinder length runs local x) — all emitting local -z, worlds from the internal xformOp evaluator, `inputs:` prefix with bare pre-21.02 fallbacks, the same per-kind brightest=1 normalization over intensity×2^exposure and linear→sRGB colors as glTF; the usdz leg pinned by a test-built 64-byte-aligned stored zip both readers accept). **USD transform animation** (`SceneLoaderUSDAnimation.swift`: the same raw-tree read **bakes** each animated prim's op stack — composed local matrix at the union of its authored sample times via `localXform(at:)`/`USDAttribute.sampled(at:)`, decomposed to TRS keys with hemisphere-continuous quats — into ONE unnamed `SceneAnimation` for the layer's whole timeline (`scene.animations.first`), so pivot pairs / single-axis / matrix ops all play; timebase = `timeCodesPerSecond` ?? `framesPerSecond` ?? 24 offset by `startTimeCode`; **interpolation is a runtime stage setting never authored in-file** — linear default, quats slerp, non-lerpable holds — so baked tracks are LINEAR; the bake returns **path-keyed entries** the scene walk maps onto `sourceIndex`, and each animated prim's decomposed rest pose installs as its node's `trs` base; crate leg pinned against **/usr/bin/usdcat** (a reference crate writer, tool-gated) baking byte-identical tracks from both containers). **USD skinning** (`SceneLoaderUSDSkinning.swift`: UsdSkel skeletons/skins/blend shapes resolved from the same raw-tree read into the shipped deforming tier — attach-only now, the walk already built every mesh. **Load-bearing:** each Skeleton prim **synthesizes joint nodes** — a container named after the prim at its evaluator world transform, one node per joint nested by the joint paths, TRS base = local rest (derived from the world-space binds when `restTransforms` is unauthored), **appended after the tree nodes with joint identities numbered past every prim index**, so SkelAnimation joint tracks, blend-shape weights tracks, and xform tracks all bind by the one identity scheme; `inverseBind = inv(bindTransform)·geomBindTransform` makes `worlds[joint]·inverseBind·p` exactly the UsdSkel equation through the shipped pose path; `elementSize` N / constant-interpolation rigid bindings / the `skel:joints` remap all resolve (past 4 influences the heaviest win); **morphs apply before the skin** (probe-pinned by the rigid-binding test)); any other `loadMesh` format degrades honestly to a single-node scene with no cameras or lights. **`drawScene(_:)`** is a save/multiply/`drawMesh`/recurse/restore walk over `modelMatrix`, so every mesh rule (materials, shadows, reflections, export/batch gating) applies with zero new rules — **byte-identical to the equivalent manual transform-stack calls**, pinned by `SceneLoaderTests`. **Per-material submeshes:** a multi-material mesh (glTF distinct-material primitives; USD **`materialBind` GeomSubsets** — first subset claims a contested face, unclaimed faces keep the mesh's own binding) carries internal **`SceneMeshPart`** index slices on its node over the **one merged vertex order**, so morphs/skins pose once and `drawScene` emits one ordinary `drawMesh` per part off the posed arrays (CoW struct copies, nothing duplicated); the public `mesh` stays whole wearing the first material (standalone draws unchanged), a single-material node carries **no parts** (the untouched byte-identical path), and **`partsVertexCount` guards a swapped mesh back to the whole-mesh draw** (the skin-alignment treatment) — parts-vs-manual byte-equality + swapped-mesh render probes in `SceneLoaderTests`. **Load-bearing:** `Mesh.loadGLTF`'s own merge loop is **untouched** (it bakes world transforms as it appends and smooths missing normals *after* baking, so rebuilding it over per-node local meshes would not be byte-identical — the two share only the `GLTFDocument` readers, and `MeshLoaderTests` pin `loadMesh` unchanged); light intensities normalize **per kind to brightest = 1** (glTF's lux/candela mean nothing without distance falloff, which Ollin's punctual lights don't model), colors re-encode linear→sRGB; the camera's `target` is the scene-bounds center projected onto the view axis (orbit-friendly), ortho `height` = 2·`ymag`; cameras/lights **ride their nodes**: the loaders attach internal node-local specs (`SceneLightSpec`/`SceneCameraSpec`, emitting down local -z, intensities normalized at load) and `scene.cameras`/`scene.lights` are computed properties re-resolving through the tree's **current** transforms on every read, so a moved carrier node — by hand or by an applied animation — carries its camera/light, while **assigning either array (tweaking one element in place counts) freezes it** to the hand-set world-space values (`fixedCameras`/`fixedLights`); `node(_:)` returns a **copy**, the **subscript writes back in place** (`scene["part"]?.rotate(dt, axis: .unitY)` spins about the node's own pivot); `Scene` takes the bare public name, so the hosts spell `some SwiftUI.Scene` (the `Image`/`Environment` precedent); the bundled demo assets are **generated by `Scripts/make-sample-scene.swift` / `make-animated-scene.swift` / `make-skinned-scene.swift` / `make-usd-scene.swift` / `make-usd-animated-scene.swift` / `make-usd-skinned-scene.swift`** (the textured-cube precedent — no third-party license). **Scene animation** — `scene.animations` (**`SceneAnimation`**, `3D/SceneAnimation.swift`) + **`apply(_:at:)`** plays the file's node-TRS keyframe tracks on the sketch clock: **absolute posing** (same time → same pose, so per-frame application converges; loop by wrapping `time` over `duration`, one-shots hold their last key per the spec's clamp), samplers per spec (STEP / LINEAR with **shortest-arc slerp** for rotations / CUBICSPLINE Hermite with segment-duration-scaled tangents and normalized quats; rotation outputs may be **normalized ints**, decoded by the spec equations). **Load-bearing:** an animated node's base is its **authored `trs` from the file's own fields — never decomposed from the matrix** (a matrix-authored node has `trs == nil` and no track touches it, per spec) plus `sourceIndex` (the file node index tracks target); the `position` setter **syncs `trs.t`** so a hand-set position survives a rotation-only track (a hand `rotate` on an animated node is overwritten by the next `apply` — standard animation semantics); tracks **sort by node index** (the determinism rule). **Skins & morph targets** (`3D/SceneSkinning.swift` + the `localMeshData` merge) — the deforming tier, posed by `drawScene` with **no new user API**: deform data rides the **`SceneNode`, not the public `Mesh`** (`skinIndex`/`vertexJoints`/`vertexWeights`/`morphTargets` internal; **`weights` public** — the hand-drivable blend-shape surface the weights animation channel also writes; node weights override the mesh's authored defaults, the spec's precedence). **Load-bearing:** joint matrices are **scene-root world × inverse bind** (one `nodeWorldTransforms()` pre-walk, only when the scene has skins — a skinless walk is structurally unchanged, byte-identity still pinned) and a skinned mesh's **own node chain is ignored** (the spec's rule): the posed mesh draws under the `drawScene` *call's* model matrix, pinned by render probe; morphs apply **before** skinning; a misaligned skin **falls back to the undeformed draw with a one-time note, never a mis-draw**; skin attributes merge **all-or-nothing across primitives** (the UV rule) and morph data drops whole on a target-count mismatch; the weights channel stores **k scalars per keyframe** (CUBICSPLINE groups all in-tangents, then all values, then all out-tangents per key — `WeightsSampler` pins the layout); `readVec3` reads **sparse and zero-filled no-buffer-view accessors** (the morph-exporter staple); vertex weights renormalize over the influences actually used, a zero-weight vertex keeps bind pose. Examples `3D/Geometry/LoadedScene` + `AnimatedScene` + `SkinnedScene` + `USDScene` + `USDAnimatedScene` + `USDSkinnedScene` (all honor env override `OLLIN_SCENE`; the animated ones declare `loopDuration`, wraps verified byte-identical — the USD ones pinned by Metal-gated render probes); snapshots `loaded-scene` + `animated-scene` + `skinned-scene` + `usd-scene` + `usd-animated-scene` + `usd-skinned-scene`; `SceneLoaderTests` + `SceneAnimationTests` + `SceneSkinningTests` + `USDAnimationTests` + `USDSkinningTests` (usdcat crate cross-check + a platform bind-matrix oracle). Mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Scene import*. `Docs/3D/Scenes.md`.

- **USD parser core (OllinUSD)** (`Sources/Ollin/3D/USD/` — internal, no public API; the substrate of the complete USD-native arc). Ollin's own clean-room reader opens all three containers — `.usda` text (recursive descent, a deliberate *superset* of the grammar), `.usdc` crate **0.8-0.10** (bootstrap + TOC sections + ValueReps, LZ4 via Apple Compression + the delta/varint integer coding), `.usdz` package (own central-directory zip; default layer = first usd-extension entry; `USDStage.open` keeps the archive + layer name so the scene loader can resolve package assets) — content-sniffed, never by extension, yielding one raw tree (`USDStage`/`USDPrim`/`USDAttribute`/`USDValue`) in **authored order** (crate: path-traversal order overridden by `primChildren`/`properties`); this tree is the *only* source the whole USD `loadScene`/`loadMesh` path reads (the scene-import bullet). Envelope: flattened single layers + self-contained packages, variants at defaults, no composition — out-of-envelope constructs (variantSets, references/payloads, splines, relocates) skip string-aware as `.unsupported`, **never a throw**. **The xformOp evaluator** (`USDXform.swift`, the raw tree's first consumer tier): `USDPrim.localXform()` + `USDStage.visitPrims` compose authored ops per `xformOpOrder` — **listed ops outermost-first** (= left-to-right column-vector composition), **row-vector `matrix4d`** (its four rows load directly as simd columns), **degrees**, a three-axis rotate's value always (x,y,z) angles with the *name* picking application order, `!invert!` + `!resetXformStack!` honored, dangling ops skipped — cross-checked against Model I/O's own composition of the example camera (`USDXformTests`). **Load-bearing:** the raw tree keeps the *file's* shape — tuple arrays flat with an arity, float arrays stay `Float` (bit-exact cross-checks), and untyped metadata differs by format (text `.stringArray` vs crate `.tokenArray` — consumers normalize, the raw tier doesn't) — with one deliberate exception: **quats arrive real-first (w, i, j, k) from both containers** (crate stores the raw struct *imaginary-first*, the one shape where the containers genuinely diverged, so the crate reader reorders scalar + array quats at decode); the crate traps (AssetPath's token-vs-string index asymmetry, Vec2h's raw-half inlining, the 64-bit integer coder's wider vint widths, TimeSamples' double indirection, payload-0 = empty array, sub-16-element "compressed" arrays stored raw) are honored in code and documented — don't re-derive them. **Verification is all local:** Model I/O exports `.usda` *and* `.usdc` (crate 0.8) from one asset — export both under **one file stem** (the root prim is named after the file) — the two independent parser paths must agree and match the Model I/O read-back; for a file Model I/O didn't author, `MDLMesh.vertexCount` is the *expanded* per-face-corner count (== our `faceVertexIndices` count), never the authored points count. System vectors: the CoreUSDEdit shaderball (crate 0.9; its authored child order — the one Model I/O alphabetizes — is pinned at both the parser and the loaded-`Scene` level), the PencilKit pen usdz set (0.8). Reparse pinned byte-deterministic. `USDParserTests`; format-study credit in `ATTRIBUTION.md`. Mechanism + format facts: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *The USD parser core*.

- **Camera control & cinematic moves.** Two opt-in halves over the shipped `Camera3D`, both driving the `Camera3D.orbiting` pose (object-on-a-turntable, not scene-flythrough) and set as the frame's camera — a sketch that calls neither keeps its own. **`cameraControl()`** hands the camera to the viewer (drag orbit, scroll dolly, right-/shift-/option-drag pan; exponential damping toward an input-driven goal + a capped momentum flick; the canonical orbit-control model, `deltaTime`-corrected, studied not ported). **`cameraMove(_:)`** plays a curated **`CameraMove`** — `.turntable` / `.sway` / `.pushIn` / `.pullOut` / `.tilt` / `.orbitAndRise` / `.reveal` / `.handheld` (cyclic ones phase-driven, finite ones on an internal `Timeline`, handheld a private Perlin field). Both seed the framing on the **first call only** and **compose over the current pose** (`CameraRig.lastMode` re-syncs the controller's goal after a move), so "frame by hand, then drift" is one call after another, and the rig clamps elevation off the poles. All three calls take a **`from: Camera3D` form** seeding the opening shot from any authored camera (a loaded scene's `scene.camera` — the "open on the authored framing, then hand the viewer the camera" one-liner): `Camera3D.orbitPose` decomposes eye/target into the orbit pose, an orthographic camera seeds the rig flat (`seed(orthographic:)`, frame height mapped to the equivalent fov at the target distance so the projection toggle holds scale), `near`/`far` default to the camera's own, and roll is dropped (the rig is y-up by design). The shared **`Timeline<Value: Tweenable>`** sequencer (`Sources/Ollin/Math/Timeline.swift` — timed keyframes + per-segment easing + `loops`, `FrameAdvancing` like `@Eased`, `Tweenable` over `Double`/`Vector2`/`Vector3`) is the substrate, available to sketches (the future `@Param` keyframe client too). The rig (`Sources/Ollin/3D/CameraRig.swift` + `CameraMove.swift`) is a plain `let` advanced explicitly, **not** `FrameAdvancing` (the Mirror-collection cache only sees pre-first-frame stored properties); its platform-neutral input surface — `scrollDeltaY` (**double-buffered** in `advance()` so a wheel event between frames is never lost), `modifiers` (mapped behind the AppKit seam like `KeyCode`), `rightMouseIsPressed`, the `mouseWheel()` hook — is plumbed once through `OllinMTKView` so it reaches standalone + gallery + OllinLive. **`cameraShowcase(_:)`** is the fused default the ~42 auto-orbit `3D/*` examples adopt: it plays a `CameraMove` the viewer can interrupt and, after `idleReturn` (10s) of no input, eases back over `returnDuration` (4s, Hermite, shortest-arc azimuth) to the **opening** framing (the `CameraRig` anchor captured at `seed()`, *not* wherever the viewer left it), then resumes — a 3-phase `driving`/`manual`/`returning` state machine, **byte-identical to a plain move when uninteracted** (so headless/export/snapshot paths are unaffected and core `cameraMove`/`camera` are untouched). `.autoOrbit(period:)` is its curated default move; `activeCamera` exposes the resolved `Camera3D` (e.g. placing geometry at the camera `eye`, as `DepthCompositing` does). **Scene inspection views** — `cameraView(_:)` / `resetCamera()` snap the rig to canonical angles (`CameraView`: `.reset` restores the opening framing; the six axis views swing only the orbit angle, keeping target + radius; `.isometric` is true isometric, `.corner` the deprecated alias), gliding by default (`animated: false` cuts) and handing the pose back to the active driver via `CameraRig.driver` on completion, so motion resumes with no jump; **byte-identical when no snap is active** (`camera-move` not re-recorded); surfaced by the host **Camera menu** (`OllinCameraCommands`, ⌘0–⌘7 in all three hosts) → `OllinActiveSketch` → `SketchRunner.requestCameraView` (applied next frame); a 2D / hand-set-`camera()` sketch ignores it. **Live-only scene chrome:** `cameraAxis()` shows the interactive orientation widget (`AxisWidget`, a SwiftUI sibling — click an axis to snap, plus reset / isometric / an Ortho⇄Perspective toggle) and `groundGrid()` a faint reference floor at y=0 (a `.grid` `PipelineKey` pass, depth-tested-but-not-writing, *outside* the deferred render graph) — host chrome like the FPS overlay, **never exported, 2D no-op**, toggleable from the Camera menu. **Load-bearing grid invariant:** the infinite-grid LOD divides the **once-computed base world-uv derivative** by each decade scale — **never `dfdx()` of a pre-scaled uv** (the derivative spike at a LOD boundary shatters the lines into a crawling dotted band, a real fixed bug); major lines are distinguished **by width in the one line color**, not a brighter color or a separate per-class fade (the old form read as stacked planes at staggered depths — don't reintroduce it). Examples `3D/SceneViews`, `3D/CameraControl` + `3D/CameraMoves` (the lower-level demos); snapshot `camera-move` (a deterministic `.turntable` — the input-driven paths are covered by the `CameraRigTests` / `TimelineTests` CPU units, since a snapshot can't drive synthetic events). Full mechanism: [`ARCHITECTURE.md`](ARCHITECTURE.md) § *Camera rig*. `Docs/3D/Camera.md`, `Docs/Helpers/Animation.md` (the `Timeline` section).

- **RGBD recordings + live USB (Record3D)** (`OllinRecord3D` satellite). `Record3DRecording` opens a Record3D `.r3d` clip and `Record3DDevice` reads a tethered phone's live USB RGBD stream — both unproject color + depth with true intrinsics into a `PointCloud` (over the core `RGBDFrame` / `CameraIntrinsics`). All Apple-native decode (a minimal ZIP reader + LZFSE + JPEG) and a clean-room `USBMux` (usbmuxd) client to TCP 1337; the formats read clean-room (Record3D's library is LGPL — read its public structure, never translate; credit as a product reference, vendor nothing). **USB only, not WiFi.** **Gotchas:** `K` is column-major at capture resolution → scale to the depth grid; the depth grid isn't in the metadata (derive from the LZFSE float count + capture aspect); usbmuxd's `PortNumber` is network byte order; the trailing live-stream slot is a JSON misc blob, *not* confidence; unprojection lands in ARKit camera space (y-negate + z-negate). `RGBDFrame` gained `depth(atNormalizedX:y:)` / `unproject(normalized:)` (hole-robust median, y-flip). **Recorded poses:** `Record3DRecording.poses` / `pose(at:)` — per-frame camera-to-world from the metadata `poses` array (`[qx, qy, qz, qw, tx, ty, tz]`, quaternion order pinned by the identity's 1 in slot 3), the sweep-fusion path for a saved clip; **a clip captured without world tracking (TrueDepth) stores every pose as the identity** — real motion can't be recovered after the fact, so probe `poses` for variation before fusing (single frames reconstruct fine). `Docs/3D/Record3D.md`, `Docs/3D/RGBD.md`; examples `3D/Record3DCloud`, `Record3DLiveCloud`, `DepthLiftedPose`.

- **iPhone capture app (Ollin Capture + `OllinPhone`).** Ollin's own iOS app (`Apps/OllinPhoneApp`, xcodegen + Xcode) streams on-device ARKit perception over USB: a 3D body skeleton (each joint a position **and** an orientation quaternion with a camera-observed flag — ARKit's rig fills unseen joints in, and those report untracked — plus the anchor's world transform standing the skeleton in the same ARKit world the depth sweep and room mesh use, and the person's estimated scale via `automaticSkeletonScaleEstimationEnabled`; bodies bundle per frame like faces, so an empty set clears a person who left), a face (deforming mesh with its topology and per-vertex texture coordinates + 52 expression blendshapes + the two eye poses and the look-at point the eyes converge on, up to 3 faces), world-facing rear-LiDAR depth (a `PointCloud` + the 6DoF camera pose, via the core `RGBDFrame`), person segmentation (a matte + cutout, via the core-lifted `SegmentationImages` — fed by the rear camera in **Segment** mode, ARKit's on-device segmentation, or by the front camera in **Selfie** mode, the app's one non-ARKit sensor: an `AVCaptureSession` + Vision pass whose capture connection rotates buffers upright (so the wire's turn count is always 0) and mirrors them like the front-camera preview — the matte is computed from the delivered buffer, so matte/color alignment is by construction, and both modes ride the same `.segmentation` wire kind so the Mac side needed nothing new), the **hands** in view (up to 4 at once, each a 21-joint skeleton found on-device by Vision over the ARKit frames in **Hands** mode — every joint an upright 2D image point, plus on a LiDAR phone a metric 3D position in ARKit world space, lifted through the scene-depth map and the camera pose), the **text** it can read (each line a `PhoneText` in **Text** mode — the string, the reader's confidence, and four upright corner points, lifted to world space the same way), the reconstructed **room mesh** with the **flat planes** in it, the **room's light**, and CoreMotion device motion. The Mac client `PhoneDevice` (`@MainActor`, self-retrying) exposes `latestBody` / `latestBodies` / `latestFaces` / `latestHands` / `latestHand` / `latestTexts` / `latestText` / `latestMotion` / `latestDepthFrame` / `latestSegmentationMatte` / `latestSegmentationCutout` / `sceneMesh` / `planes` / `latestLight`; `PhoneBody` / `PhoneFace` / `PhoneHand` / `PhoneMotion` mirror the Vision accessor shape; `PhoneBody` adds `worldPosition(_:)`/`worldCenter`/`orientation(_:)`/`modelTransform(_:)`/`worldTransform(of:)`/`scaleFactor`/`isJointTracked(_:)` and a **public init** so a pose stages with no phone (the tests and the staged-figure verification do). Transport: the shared `OllinUSBMux` tunnel + the `PhoneWire` codec (pure Foundation/simd, compiled verbatim into both ends; `streamPort` 1338). **World-pose fusion** — the core `WorldCloud` accumulator places each depth frame by its pose into one fixed world cloud (sweep the phone, fuse the room); see the *Fusing a sweep* bullet for it and for the drift correction that keeps a long one registered. **Room mesh** — ARKit scene reconstruction streamed *per anchor block* (`.sceneMesh`, one block per message, keyed by a stable `UUID`, with a `removed` retirement notice); `PhoneSceneMesh` keeps the newest of each in arrival order (`apply`/`remove`/`reset`) and builds `mesh` (whole room, world space), `mesh(of:)` (filtered by label, unreached vertices dropped), and `mesh(colored:)` (unwelded, a color per triangle); `PhoneSurface` is the per-triangle label (wall/floor/ceiling/table/seat/window/door/unclassified, raw values contiguous from 0). **Load-bearing:** the phone reads a block's geometry **inside** the ARKit callback (the Metal buffers are the session's, and nothing promises they outlive the call) and throttles the *send* instead (a FIFO queue, ~15 blocks/s, so a block that stops changing still gets its turn); a geometry source is read three floats at a time honoring its own `stride` (a `SIMD3<Float>` is 16 bytes over a 12-byte entry and would run past the end); `phoneSceneChunk` places positions with w=1 and normals with w=0, and **refuses a whole block whose index runs past its vertices**; `sceneMeshVersion` is the rebuild gate (a room is hundreds of thousands of triangles, so `draw()` must not rebuild per frame); removal rebuilds the id→slot map, since every later block shifts down; and each block carries a per-run **`scan`** number, because `session.run(…, .resetTracking)` moves the world origin, so the Mac must drop the old room rather than mix two coordinate spaces (leaving Room mode and returning is a new run). **Flat planes** — ARKit plane anchors stream beside the mesh from the *same* session (`.plane`, one anchor per message, same `id`/`scan`/`removed` shape, its own FIFO ~20/s), so surface and planes share one world origin; `PhonePlane` carries the label (the same `PhoneSurface`), `PhonePlaneAlignment` (horizontal/vertical), world-space `center`/`normal`/`width`/`height`, the convex `boundary`, plus `area`/`mesh`/`outline`/`bounds`; `PhonePlanes` is the id-keyed set (`apply`/`remove`/`reset`) with `flat`/`upright`/`planes(of:)`/`largest`/`largest(of:)`/`floor`/`foundSurfaces`/`bounds`/`mesh(colored:)`. **Load-bearing:** plane detection needs **no LiDAR**, so the Room session starts either way and adds `sceneReconstruction` only when supported (the mode is useful on a non-Pro phone); `phonePlane` **reverses an outline whose winding disagrees with the anchor's up axis** (a fan built the wrong way faces backwards and the fill vanishes; `anOutlineWoundTheWrongWayIsTurnedRound` pins it, verified red by sabotage) and **synthesizes a rectangle from `planeExtent` when no outline arrives** (turned by `rotationOnYAxis`), so every reader downstream has exactly one shape to handle; `largest` measures the **outline's** area (a fan of cross products), never `width × height`, or a long thin shelf outranks a floor; `floor` falls back to the lowest horizontal plane, because a label arrives late. **Room light** — `frame.lightEstimate` in **every** mode via the shared `LightSampler` (a `LightReporting` protocol on all six ARKit streamers, throttled to 5/s), so a mode switch never interrupts it; `PhoneLight` scales lumens to `intensity` (1000 lm = 1), turns `kelvin` into `color` (`Color(kelvin:)`) and `ambient` (that color scaled by intensity, clamped at 1.5 for `ambientLight(_:)`), and exposes `direction`/`keyIntensity`/`sphericalHarmonics`/`key` **only in Face mode** (`ARDirectionalLightEstimate` is face-tracking-only, 27 coefficients as raw `Data`). `PhonePlane` and `PhoneLight` both take a **public init** so a room and a reading can be staged with no phone (the Guide figure and the tests do). **The eyes and the gaze** — `PhoneFace` adds `eyePosition(_:)`/`eyeOrientation(_:)`/`gazeDirection(_:)` (face-local, per `PhoneEye`), their world twins `worldEyePosition(_:)`/`worldGazeDirection(_:)`, `lookAtPoint`/`worldLookAtPoint` (the convergence point), `headTransform` (the head pose composed for `transform(_:)`, so the face-local mesh stands with the eyes inside it), and `meshUVs`; `mesh()` carries the texture coordinates only when they align with the vertices (a misaligned set is dropped, never shifted onto the wrong vertices), and a **public init** stages a face with no phone (the Guide figure and the tests do). **Load-bearing:** the eye poses and `lookAtPoint` ride the wire **face-local** (the SDK's own convention — relative to the anchor's origin), stood in world through the head pose; the gaze axis is the eye's **+z** (out of the face; a device-unverified knob — if a live run reads backward, flip it in `gazeDirection`, one place). **Hands** — `PhoneHand` carries `chirality` (from the camera's view) / `confidence` / per-joint `has(_:)`/`confidence(of:)`, the 2D overlay (`point(_:in:)`/`points(in:)`/`bones(in:)`, upright normalized on the wire whichever way the phone is held), the 3D side (`position(_:)`/`bones()`/`cloud()`/`center`/`hasWorldPositions`), the gesture staple `pinchDistance` (thumb tip to index tip, meters, `nil` unless both lifted), and the static `skeleton` (20 bones) / `tips` / `fingerChains`; a **public init** stages a hand with no phone (the staged-render verification and the tests do). **Load-bearing:** the pose model runs behind a **drop-if-busy gate** on its own queue (a slow pass skips frames, never backs up the camera) and everything the lift needs is **copied inside the ARKit callback** (the room-mesh lesson); the model is handed the device-hold orientation and its points come back **upright**, which is what the wire carries, and the 3D lift maps each point back onto the camera-native depth grid through `PhoneWire.bufferPoint(fromUpright:quarterTurnsCW:)` — pure shared arithmetic, pinned by a Mac test (`mapsUprightPointsOntoTheBuffer`) — then takes a **median depth** from a small window (a fingertip's own pixel is often a hole at the silhouette edge), unprojects through the depth-grid intrinsics, and applies the camera pose; a joint over a depth hole keeps its 2D point with `hasWorldPosition` off, so `position(_:)` is honest about what lifted; the empty set clears hands that left (the faces precedent); `PhoneHandJoint` raw values stay contiguous 0…20 (the wire carries a joint as one byte; pinned like the blendshapes); without LiDAR the mode still runs and hands stay 2D. **Text** — `PhoneText` carries `text`/`confidence`, the 2D overlay (`corners(in:)`/`bounds(in:)`, upright normalized on the wire), and the world side (`worldCorners`/`worldCenter`/`worldWidth`/`worldHeight`/`hasWorldPlacement`, plus `worldTransform` — an orthonormalized frame for `transform(_:)`: origin at the quad's center, x along the reading direction, y up the line, z out of the surface, `nil` on a degenerate quad); a **public init** stages a line with no phone (the Guide figure and the tests do). **Load-bearing:** `TextStreamer` mirrors the hand streamer exactly (its own queue behind the drop-if-busy gate, everything the lift needs copied inside the ARKit callback, the device-hold orientation handed to `VNRecognizeTextRequest`, corners lifted through the shared depth arithmetic — now extracted to the app's `DepthLift.swift`, which both streamers use, with the median window clamping its pixel first so an edge corner can't build an inverted range); a line lifts **all four corners or none** (three corners are not a quad; the all-or-nothing normals precedent); it reads at the `.accurate` recognition level (`.fast` misses room-scale text; a few finished readings a second, absorbed by the gate); `latestText` ranks by `imageArea` (the shoelace area of the normalized quad — recognizer confidences cluster near 1, so area picks the sign you mean); the empty set clears lines that left (the faces precedent); the placement test is sabotage-verified (a flipped normal turns it red); the worldTransform z axis faces **out of the surface toward the reader** (a device-unverified knob — if a live run reads backward, flip the cross in `worldTransform`, one place). Body / Face / World / Segment / Selfie / Room / Hands / Text are behind a phone-side camera toggle (mutually exclusive; motion always streams; light streams in every ARKit mode — Selfie runs no ARKit session and sends none; eight modes outgrew the segmented control, so the app draws two rows of chips). **Gotchas:** the segmentation upright-rotation must be an accelerated `CGContext` draw, not a byte loop (debug-build frame rate); the 52 blendshapes ride the wire positionally so `PhoneBlendShape` raw values must stay contiguous 0…51; `PhoneWire.version` stays 1 pre-1.0 (both ends rebuild together). `Docs/3D/Phone.md`; examples `3D/PhoneBodyPose` / `PhoneBodyFigure` (a solid mannequin posed by the joint orientations, standing where the person stands) / `PhoneCostume` (ribbon trails + rotation-twisted plumage worn by the live skeleton; header credits its Super You inspiration) / `PhoneFace` / `PhoneGaze` (eyeballs at the streamed eye poses, beams converging on the look-at bead) / `PhoneDepthCloud` / `PhoneWorldScan` / `PhoneSegmentation` / `PhoneRoomMesh` / `PhoneRoomPlanes` / `PhoneHands` (the hands as solid little skeletons in the room, a pinch closing into a bead, a 2D overlay without LiDAR) / `PhoneWorldText` (each readable line as wire-frame stroke type on a framed panel, standing where it hangs; a 2D overlay without LiDAR); `PhoneSceneMeshTests` (20), `PhonePlanesTests` (26), `PhoneBodyTests` (6), `PhoneFaceTests` (7), `PhoneHandTests` (8), `PhoneTextTests` (8); Guide Ch 26 figures `RoomAsSurface` + `RoomAsPlanes` + `HandsAsSkeletons` + `GazeAsBeams` + `WordsInPlace`.

- **Fusing a sweep, and keeping it registered** (`Sources/Ollin/3D/WorldCloud.swift` + `3D/CloudRegistration.swift`, core, source-agnostic). `WorldCloud` is a voxel accumulator: `add(_:transformedBy:)` places a camera-space cloud by its pose and keeps one point per cube, so re-seeing a wall refreshes it in place and the cloud is bounded by surface area rather than frame count; `PointCloud.transformed(by:)` and `Vector3.transformed(by:)` are the placement primitives. `add(_:correcting:)` / `align(_:from:)` line the frame up against what is already fused *before* merging, which is what stops a long sweep smearing (a tracker's per-frame error never cancels, so it grows into tens of centimetres); the fix is kept in `correction` and returned as `CloudAlignment` (pose, correction, overlap, error, passes, applied). Frame-to-model point-to-plane ICP, implemented from Low, UNC TR04-004 (2004). **Load-bearing:** the recovered angles must rebuild a **true** rotation matrix, never the linearized one the system was built from (it is not a rotation, so feeding it back swells the scan a little every round) and **only `solverAnswersWithATrueRotation` catches this**, because at a well-tracked frame's angles the two matrices agree; the 6x6 normal equations are solved through their eigenvectors with a pseudo-inverse threshold, so a direction the geometry does not pin down is answered with zero instead of a huge number (one blank wall corrects across itself and never slides along itself); `correction` is the next frame's starting guess, which is what leaves only the newest error to find and lets one ring of cubes be enough; correspondence is *approximate by design*, the cube holding the point answers in one hash lookup (the grid keeps one point per cube) and the wider ring is paid only by misses; normals are fitted per voxel on demand and cached, widening to two rings when the neighborhood is thin (a feed sparser than the grid gets none at one ring, found by test); **overlap counts points that found a surface, not points that got a normal** (conflating them makes the guard refuse good frames); the step's turn is read from sine *and* cosine, never the trace alone (the trace is flat near zero, so a convergence test on it can never pass in single precision); a fit with too little overlap or one asking for a jump is refused and the cloud goes in at the fix *earlier* frames established, never at the raw reported pose. Measured at about 5 ms per round in the debug build against a 46k-point model at 1200 samples, roughly half what merging the frame costs. **The pseudo-inverse cutoff is a conditioning threshold, not a numerical one** (`unmeasured = 1e-3` of the largest eigenvalue): set near the floating-point floor the solve answers a direction the geometry does not pin down at all, and a camera facing one flat wall slides along it a few cm a frame while overlap and leftover error both stay perfect (measured: 0.416 m of slide over 20 frames became 0.011 m when the cutoff moved from 1e-8 to 1e-3). `CloudAlignment.stability` reports the smallest of the six eigenvalues over the largest (Gelfand et al., 3DIM 2003) and `Settings.minimumStability` refuses on it (default 0, so the live path is unchanged; the loop matcher raises it). `Docs/3D/Phone.md` (World fusion, Keeping a long sweep registered); examples `3D/Depth/DriftCorrectedScan` (no hardware, side by side) and `3D/Phone/PhoneWorldScan` (**C** cycles it); `WorldCloudTests` (8) + `CloudRegistrationTests` (15), all GPU-free.
